import os import time import subprocess from rich.live import Live from rich.table import Table from rich.layout import Layout from rich.panel import Panel from rich.console import Console # Config BUS_DIR = "/storage/emulated/0/RootBase/modules/ccb_workbench_v1/.grimoire/bus" LOG_DIR = "/data/data/com.termux/files/home/MikeySwarm/logs/archon" CONSOLE = Console() def get_bus_status(): table = Table(title="Agent Bus Status") table.add_column("Agent", style="cyan") table.add_column("Last Task", style="green") files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(BUS_DIR) if f.endswith('.json')], key=lambda f: os.path.getmtime(os.path.join(BUS_DIR, f)), reverse=True) for f in files[:5]: agent = f.split('_')[0] table.add_row(agent, "Active" if "completed" not in f else "Done") return table def get_log_stream(): logs = "" try: files = sorted([os.path.join(LOG_DIR, f) for f in os.listdir(LOG_DIR)], key=os.path.getmtime, reverse=True) with open(files[0], 'r') as f: logs = "".join(f.readlines()[-10:]) except: logs = "No logs found." return Panel(logs, title="Archon Logs") def generate_layout(): layout = Layout() layout.split_column( Layout(name="header", size=3), Layout(name="body") ) layout["body"].split_row( Layout(name="bus"), Layout(name="logs") ) layout["header"].update(Panel("CCB Workbench Dashboard", style="bold white on blue")) layout["bus"].update(get_bus_status()) layout["logs"].update(get_log_stream()) return layout with Live(generate_layout(), refresh_per_second=1) as live: while True: time.sleep(1) live.update(generate_layout())

import subprocess import json import os import sys import datetime # Mock imports for bridge integration - update paths as needed GUARD = "/storage/emulated/0/RootBase/modules/ccb_workbench_v1/core/security/guard.py" AST_BRIDGE = "/storage/emulated/0/RootBase/modules/ccb_workbench_v1/core/syntax/ast_bridge.py" ARCHON_LOGS = "/data/data/com.termux/files/home/MikeySwarm/logs/archon" class JanusEngine: def __init__(self): self.max_retries = 3 def log_event(self, event_data): timestamp = datetime.datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S") with open(f"{ARCHON_LOGS}/janus_{timestamp}.json", "w") as f: json.dump(event_data, f) def secure_execute(self, script_path): # 1. Security Gate print(f"Securing: {script_path}") guard_res = subprocess.run(['python3', GUARD, script_path], capture_output=True, text=True) if guard_res.returncode != 0: self.log_event({"action": "reject", "script": script_path, "reason": guard_res.stderr}) return False, "Security Violation" # 2. Execution Loop for attempt in range(self.max_retries): print(f"Executing attempt {attempt+1}...") proc = subprocess.run(['python3', script_path], capture_output=True, text=True) if proc.returncode == 0: self.log_event({"action": "success", "script": script_path, "attempt": attempt+1}) return True, proc.stdout # 3. Heal (Exception Parsing) print(f"Failure detected: {proc.stderr}") self.log_event({"action": "retry", "script": script_path, "stderr": proc.stderr, "attempt": attempt+1}) # Placeholder for AST-patching logic return False, "Failed after max retries" if __name__ == "__main__": if len(sys.argv) > 1: engine = JanusEngine() success, output = engine.secure_execute(sys.argv[1]) print(output)

import yfinance as yf import pandas as pd import sys def get_control_metrics(ticker): try: data = yf.Ticker(ticker).history(period="1mo") if data.empty: return None trend = "BULLISH" if data['Close'].iloc[-1] > data['Close'].iloc[0] else "BEARISH" volatility = data['Close'].std() return { "ticker": ticker, "trend": trend, "volatility": float(volatility), "source": "yfinance_foss_control" } except Exception as e: return {"error": str(e)} if __name__ == "__main__": ticker = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "TSLA" print(get_control_metrics(ticker))

filename.endswith('.tsx') or filename.endswith('.jsx'): return 'react_dome' elif filename.endswith('.ts') or filename.endswith('.js'): return 'javascript_spire' else: return 'data_bunker' def get_file_color(self, filename): """Get building color based on file type""" colors = { 'python_tower': '#3572A5', Python blue 'react_dome': '#61DAFB', React blue 'javascript_spire': '#F7DF1E', JS yellow 'data_bunker': '#4CAF50' Green } return colors.get(self.get_file_type(filename), '#666666') def scan_file_for_errors(self, filepath): """Scan file for errors and return severity level""" try: with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f: content = f.read() error_level = 0 # Check for common error patterns error_patterns = [ ('SyntaxError', 3), ('NameError', 2), ('TypeError', 2), ('IndexError', 2), ('TODO', 1), ('FIXME', 1), ('HACK', 1), ('XXX', 1) ] for pattern, severity in error_patterns: if pattern.lower() in content.lower(): error_level += severity # Check for long files random.randint(-5, 5), 'y': random.randint(10, building['height']), 'z': building['z'] + random.randint(-5, 5) }, 'particles': random.randint(10, 50) } self.disasters.append(fire) def """ import json import random import os import sys from datetime import datetime from flask import Flask, render_template_string from flask_socketio import SocketIO, emit from watchdog.observers import Observer from watchdog.events import FileSystemEventHandler app = Flask(__name__) socketio = SocketIO(app, cors_allowed_origins="*") class CodeCityApocalypse: def __init__(self): self.buildings = [] self.disasters = [] self.monsters = [] self.air_raids = [] self.last_scan = {} def scan_project(self, project_path="."): """Scan project, update only changed files for real-time feel""" updated = False for root, dirs, files in os.walk(project_path): for file in files: if file.endswith(('.py', '.ts', '.tsx', '.js')): filepath = os.path.join(root, file) mtime = os.path.getmtime(filepath) if filepath not in self.last_scan or self.last_scan[filepath] != mtime: self.last_scan[filepath] = mtime updated = True # Update or add building building = next((b for b in self.buildings if b['path'] == filepath), None) if not building: building = { 'id': len(self.buildings), 'name': file, 'path': filepath, 'x': random.randint(0, 100), 'z': random.randint(0, 100), } self.buildings.append(building) size = os.path.getsize(filepath) building.update({ 'height': max(10, min(100, size // 1000)), 'width': random.randint(5, 15), 'depth': random.randint(5, 15), 'health': 100, 'type': self.get_file_type(file), 'color': self.get_file_color(file), 'windows': random.randint(5, 50), 'occupants': random.randint(1, 10) }) error_level = self.scan_file_for_errors(filepath) if error_level > 0: building['health'] = max(0, 100 - (error_level * 20)) self.trigger_disaster(building, error_level) if updated: socketio.emit('update_scene', self.generate_city_scene()) return self.generate_city_scene() # ... OSError as e: print(f"Skipped {filepath}: {e}") continue if file_count >= 500: break print(f"Scan complete: {file_count} files.") return self.generate_city_scene() def get_file_type(self, filename): if filename.endswith('.py'): return 'python_tower' elif filename.endswith('.tsx') or filename.endswith('.jsx'): return 'react_dome' elif filename.endswith('.ts') or filename.endswith('.js'): return 'javascript_spire' else: return 'data_bunker' def get_file_color(self, filename): colors = { 'python_tower': '#3572A5', 'react_dome': '#61DAFB', 'javascript_spire': '#F7DF1E', 'data_bunker': '#4CAF50' } return colors.get(self.get_file_type(filename), '#666666') def scan_file_for_errors(self, filepath): try: with open(filepath, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f: content = f.read() error_level = 0 patterns = [ ('SyntaxError', 3), ('NameError', 2), ('TypeError', 2), ('IndexError', 2), ('TODO', 1), ('FIXME', 1), ('HACK', 1), ('XXX', 1) ] for pattern, severity in patterns: if pattern.lower() in content.lower(): error_level += severity lines = content.split('\n') if len(lines) > 200: error_level += 1 if len(lines) > 500: error_level += 2 return min(5, error_level) except Exception as e: print(f"Error scanning {filepath}: {e}") return 3 def trigger_disaster(self, building, severity): disasters = [self.create_fire, self.create_monster, self.create_air_raid] for _ in range(severity): random.choice(disasters)(building) def create_fire(self, building): fire = { 'type': 'fire', 'building_id': building['id'], 'intensity': random.randint(1, 10), 'position': {'x': building['x'] + random.randint(-5, 5), 'y': random.randint(10, building['height']), 'z': building['z'] + random.randint(-5, 5)}, 'particles': random.randint(10, 50) } self.disasters.append(fire) def create_monster(self, building): monster = { 'type': 'monster', 'species': random.choice(['gorilla', 'lizard', 'wolf', 'dinosaur']), 'building_id': building['id'], 'position': {'x': building['x'], 'y': 0, 'z': building['z']}, 'scale': random.uniform(1.0, 3.0), 'health': random.randint(50, 100), 'damage': random.randint(1, 5) } self.monsters.append(monster) def create_air_raid(self, building): plane = { 'type': 'air_raid', 'pilot': 'red_baron', 'building_id': building['id'], 'position': {'x': random.randint(0, 100), 'y': random.randint(30, 50), 'z': random.randint(0, 100)}, 'velocity': {'x': random.uniform(-2, 2), 'y': 0, 'z': random.uniform(-2, 2)}, 'bombs': random.randint(1, 5) } self.air_raids.append(plane) def generate_city_scene(self): return { 'metadata': { 'generated_at': datetime.now().isoformat(), 'total_buildings': len(self.buildings), 'total_disasters': len(self.disasters), 'total_monsters': len(self.monsters), 'total_air_raids': len(self.air_raids) }, 'city': { 'buildings': self.buildings, 'disasters': self.disasters, 'monsters': self.monsters, 'air_raids': self.air_raids } } def generate_html(self, scene_data): html = f""" <!DOCTYPE html> <html> <head> <title>Code City Apocalypse</title> <style> body {{ margin: 0; background: #1a1a2e; color: white; font-family: monospace; }} #info {{ position: absolute; top: 10px; left: 10px; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.8); padding: 10px; }} #stats {{ position: absolute; top: 10px; right: 10px; background: rgba(0,0,0,0.8); padding: 10px; }} </style> <script src="https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/three.js/r128/three.min.js"></script> </head> <body> <div id="info"> <h2>🏙️ CODE CITY APOCALYPSE</h2> <p>Buildings = Files | Disasters = Errors</p> </div> <div id="stats"> <h3>Stats</h3> <p>Buildings: {scene_data['metadata']['total_buildings']}</p> <p>Disasters: {scene_data['metadata']['total_disasters']}</p> </div> <script> const scene = new THREE.Scene(); const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(75, window.innerWidth / window.innerHeight, 0.1, 1000); const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer(); renderer.setSize(window.innerWidth, window.innerHeight); document.body.appendChild(renderer.domElement); scene.add(new THREE.AmbientLight(0x404040)); const ground = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.PlaneGeometry(200, 200), new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({{ color: 0x2d5a27 }})); ground.rotation.x = -Math.PI / 2; scene.add(ground); const cityData = {json.dumps(scene_data)}; cityData.city.buildings.forEach(b => {{ const geom = new THREE.BoxGeometry(b.width, b.height, b.depth); const mat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({{ color: b.color }}); const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geom, mat); mesh.position.set(b.x, b.height / 2, b.z); scene.add(mesh); }}); camera.position.set(50, 50, 50); camera.lookAt(0, 0, 0); function animate() {{ requestAnimationFrame(animate); renderer.render(scene, camera); }} animate(); </script> </body> </html> """ return html def main(): apocalypse = CodeCityApocalypse() path = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else "." scene_data = apocalypse.scan_project(path) html_content = apocalypse.generate_html(scene_data) print("HTML generated. Copy-paste the below into a file named 'code_city.html' and open in a browser:") print(html_content) # Also try to save it try: with open('code_city.html', 'w') as f: f.write(html_content) print("File saved as 'code_city.html' in current directory.") except Exception as e: print(f"Couldn't save file: {e}") if __name__ == "__main__": main()

""" [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: code_city_apocalypse_v2.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:12:53Z [/DNA_TAG] """ #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ CODE CITY APOCALYPSE - Visualizes errors as building fires, monsters, and aerial attacks """ import json import random import time from datetime import datetime class CodeCityApocalypse: def __init__(self): self.buildings = [] # Files as buildings self.disasters = [] # Active disasters self.monsters = [] # Rampage monsters self.planes = [] # Red Baron flybys self.error_count = 0 # Disaster types with ASCII art self.disaster_types = { "syntax_error": {"name": "Building Fire", "symbol": "🔥", "severity": 2}, "runtime_error": {"name": "Monster Attack", "symbol": "👾", "severity": 3}, "import_error": {"name": "Red Baron Strike", "symbol": "✈️", "severity": 4}, "logic_error": {"name": "Earthquake", "symbol": "🌋", "severity": 3}, "memory_error": {"name": "Alien Invasion", "symbol": "🛸", "severity": 5} } # Monster types (Rampage style) self.monster_types = [ {"name": "George", "symbol": "🦍", "health": 100, "attack": "SMASH"}, {"name": "Lizzie", "symbol": "🦖", "health": 150, "attack": "CHOMP"}, {"name": "Ralph", "symbol": "🐺", "health": 80, "attack": "SLASH"} ] def scan_project(self, directory="."): """Scan project and create code city""" import os self.buildings = [] for root, dirs, files in os.walk(directory): for file in files: if file.endswith(('.py', '.ts', '.tsx', '.js')): building = { "name": file, "path": os.path.join(root, file), "height": min(50, os.path.getsize(os.path.join(root, file)) // 100), # Height based on file size "health": 100, "errors": [], "last_modified": datetime.fromtimestamp(os.path.getmtime(os.path.join(root, file))), "symbol": "🏢" if file.endswith('.py') else "🏬" } self.buildings.append(building) print(f"🏙️ CODE CITY BUILT: {len(self.buildings)} buildings") return self.buildings def trigger_disaster(self, error_type, file_path, error_message): """Trigger a disaster in the code city""" disaster = self.disaster_types.get(error_type, self.disaster_types["syntax_error"]) # Find the building (file) target_building = None for building in self.buildings: if building["path"] == file_path: target_building = building break if not target_building: # Create a new building for this error target_building = { "name": file_path.split('/')[-1], "path": file_path, "height": 30, "health": 100, "errors": [], "symbol": "🏚️" } self.buildings.append(target_building) # Add error to building target_building["errors"].append({ "type": error_type, "message": error_message, "timestamp": datetime.now(), "disaster": disaster }) # Damage building damage = disaster["severity"] * 10 target_building["health"] = max(0, target_building["health"] - damage) # Add to active disasters disaster_event = { "type": error_type, "building": target_building["name"], "disaster": disaster, "start_time": datetime.now(), "position": random.randint(0, len(self.buildings) - 1) } self.disasters.append(disaster_event) self.error_count += 1 print(f"💥 DISASTER: {disaster['name']} at {target_building['name']}! Health: {target_building['health']}%") # Chance to spawn monster on big errors if disaster["severity"] >= 3 and random.random() > 0.7: self.spawn_monster(target_building) # Chance for Red Baron flyby on import errors if error_type == "import_error" and random.random() > 0.8: self.red_baron_attack() return disaster_event def spawn_monster(self, building): """Spawn a Rampage-style monster""" monster = random.choice(self.monster_types).copy() monster["target_building"] = building["name"] monster["position"] = self.buildings.index(building) monster["last_attack"] = datetime.now() self.monsters.append(monster) print(f"👾 MONSTER SPAWN: {monster['name']} {monster['symbol']} attacking {building['name']}!") return monster def red_baron_attack(self): """Red Baron aerial attack""" plane = { "name": "Red Baron", "symbol": "✈️", "position": 0, "direction": 1, "bombs_dropped": 0 } self.planes.append(plane) print(f"✈️ RED BARON INBOUND! Air raid on Code City!") return plane def update_city(self): """Update the city state - monsters attack, planes fly, disasters spread""" # Monsters attack buildings for monster in self.monsters[:]: if monster["position"] < len(self.buildings): building = self.buildings[monster["position"]] damage = random.randint(5, 15) building["health"] = max(0, building["health"] - damage) print(f"{monster['symbol']} {monster['name']} {monster['attack']} {building['name']}! -{damage}%") # Monster dies if building collapses or random chance if building["health"] <= 0 or random.random() > 0.95: print(f"💀 {monster['name']} was defeated!") self.monsters.remove(monster) # Planes fly and bomb for plane in self.planes[:]: plane["position"] += plane["direction"] # Drop bomb randomly if random.random() > 0.7 and plane["position"] < len(self.buildings): building = self.buildings[plane["position"]] damage = random.randint(10, 25) building["health"] = max(0, building["health"] - damage) plane["bombs_dropped"] += 1 print(f"💣 {plane['symbol']} BOMB DROP on {building['name']}! -{damage}%") # Plane leaves screen if plane["position"] >= len(self.buildings): print(f"✈️ {plane['name']} flies off into the sunset!") self.planes.remove(plane) # Disasters eventually end for disaster in self.disasters[:]: if (datetime.now() - disaster["start_time"]).seconds > 10: # 10 second disasters print(f"✅ {disaster['disaster']['name']} at {disaster['building']} contained!") self.disasters.remove(disaster) def render_city(self): """Render the code city with ASCII art""" print("\n" + "="*80) print("🏙️ CODE CITY APOCALYPSE - REAL-TIME VISUALIZATION") print("="*80) # City skyline city_line = [] for i, building in enumerate(self.buildings): # Building character based on health if building["health"] <= 0: symbol = "💀" # Destroyed elif building["health"] < 30: symbol = "🔥" # On fire elif building["health"] < 60: symbol = "🚒" # Damaged else: symbol = building["symbol"] # Add disasters and monsters at this position for disaster in self.disasters: if disaster["position"] == i: symbol = disaster["disaster"]["symbol"] for monster in self.monsters: if monster["position"] == i: symbol = monster["symbol"] for plane in self.planes: if plane["position"] == i: symbol = plane["symbol"] city_line.append(symbol) print("CITY: " + " ".join(city_line)) # Building status print("\n🏢 BUILDING STATUS:") for building in self.buildings[:10]: # Show first 10 health_bar = "█" * (building["health"] // 10) + "░" * (10 - (building["health"] // 10)) print(f" {building['symbol']} {building['name']:20} [{health_bar}] {building['health']}%") # Active disasters if self.disasters: print("\n💥 ACTIVE DISASTERS:") for disaster in self.disasters: print(f" {disaster['disaster']['symbol']} {disaster['disaster']['name']} at {disaster['building']}") # Monsters if self.monsters: print("\n👾 RAMPAGE MONSTERS:") for monster in self.monsters: print(f" {monster['symbol']} {monster['name']} attacking {monster['target_building']} (HP: {monster['health']})") # Planes if self.planes: print("\n✈️ AERIAL ATTACKS:") for plane in self.planes: print(f" {plane['symbol']} {plane['name']} - Bombs: {plane['bombs_dropped']}") print(f"\n📊 CITY STATS: Buildings: {len(self.buildings)} | Errors: {self.error_count} | Active Disasters: {len(self.disasters)}") print("="*80) def monitor_errors(self): """Monitor for errors and trigger disasters""" print("👁️ CODE CITY APOCALYPSE MONITOR ACTIVATED!") print("Watching for errors to turn into disasters...") # Simulate error monitoring error_simulation = [ ("syntax_error", "auth.py", "Invalid syntax on line 42"), ("import_error", "api_server.py", "Module not found: fastapi"), ("runtime_error", "dashboard.py", "Division by zero at line 15"), ("logic_error", "form_handler.py", "Infinite loop detected"), ("memory_error", "data_processor.py", "Out of memory") ] for error_type, file_path, message in error_simulation: time.sleep(3) # Wait between errors self.trigger_disaster(error_type, file_path, message) self.update_city() self.render_city() # Keep updating for ongoing chaos for _ in range(5): time.sleep(2) self.update_city() self.render_city() def main(): apocalypse = CodeCityApocalypse() # Build the city from current project apocalypse.scan_project(".") # Start monitoring for errors apocalypse.monitor_errors() if __name__ == "__main__": main()

""" [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: code_city_apocalypse_v4.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:12:53Z [/DNA_TAG] """ #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ CODE CITY APOCALYPSE v1 - Turns errors into visual chaos Buildings = Files, Errors = Disasters, Bugs = Monsters Enhanced with interactive UI and fixes. """ import json import random import math import os import sys from datetime import datetime class CodeCityApocalypse: def __init__(self): self.buildings = [] # Files as buildings self.disasters = [] # Errors as disasters self.monsters = [] # Bugs as monsters self.air_raids = [] # Syntax errors as air raids def scan_project(self, project_path="."): """Scan project and create city layout""" self.buildings = [] building_id = 0 try: for root, dirs, files in os.walk(project_path): for file in files: if file.endswith(('.py', '.ts', '.tsx', '.js')): filepath = os.path.join(root, file) try: size = os.path.getsize(filepath) except OSError: continue # Skip inaccessible files # Create building from file building = { 'id': building_id, 'name': file, 'path': filepath, 'height': max(10, min(100, size // 1000)), # Height based on file size 'width': random.randint(5, 15), 'depth': random.randint(5, 15), 'x': random.randint(0, 100), 'z': random.randint(0, 100), 'health': 100, 'type': self.get_file_type(file), 'color': self.get_file_color(file), 'windows': random.randint(5, 50), # Lines of code 'occupants': random.randint(1, 10) # Functions/classes } # Check for errors in file filename.endswith('.py'): return 'python_tower' elif filename.endswith('.tsx') or filename.endswith('.jsx'): return 'react_dome' elif filename.endswith('.ts') or filename.endswith('.js'): return 'javascript_spire' else: return 'data_bunker' def get_file_color(self, filename): """Get building color based on file type""" colors = { 'python_tower': '#3572A5', # Python blue 'react_dome': patterns error_patterns = [ ('SyntaxError', 3), ('NameError', 2), ('TypeError', 2), ('IndexError', 2), ('TODO', 1), ('FIXME', 1), ('HACK', 1), ('XXX', 1) ] for pattern, severity in error_patterns: if import random class BreedingStation: def __init__(self, parent_hives, tier_mutation_rates): self.parent_hives = parent_hives # List of hives to breed from self.tier_mutation_rates = tier_mutation_rates # Mutation rates based on tiers def hive_breed(self, n=2): """Perform N-parent crossover among parent hives.""" # Simple N-parent crossover logic offspring = [] for _ in range(n): chosen_parents = random.sample(self.parent_hives, n) # Generate offspring from chosen parents new_individual = self.cross_over(chosen_parents) offspring.append(new_individual) return offspring def _get_mutation_rate(self, difficulty): """Determine the mutation rate based on difficulty and tier.""" return self.tier_mutation_rates.get(difficulty, 0.01) def _mutate_offspring(self, offspring, difficulty): """Mutate the offspring based on difficulty tier.""" mutation_rate = self._get_mutation_rate(difficulty) for individual in offspring: if random.random() < mutation_rate: self.apply_mutation(individual) def apply_mutation(self, individual): """Apply various mutations to an individual based on predefined methods.""" # Example mutation methods to be defined self.mutate_parameters(individual) self.mutate_skills(individual) self.mutate_architecture(individual) def mutate_parameters(self, individual): """Example method to mutate parameters.""" # Implement parameter mutation logic pass def mutate_skills(self, individual): """Example method to mutate skills.""" # Implement skill mutation logic pass def mutate_architecture(self, individual): """Example method to mutate architecture.""" # Implement architecture mutation logic pass

class MutationValidator: TIER_CONSTRAINTS = { 0: {"type": "parameter", "difficulty": "Easy"}, 1: {"type": "skill", "difficulty": "Medium"}, 2: {"type": "architecture", "difficulty": "Medium"}, 3: {"type": "architecture", "difficulty": "Hard"} } def __init__(self, tier): if tier not in self.TIER_CONSTRAINTS: raise ValueError("Invalid tier: {}. """ VectorMemory: Lightweight semantic memory for agents using FAISS Stores successful code mutations and agent decisions for cross-session retrieval """ import json import os from typing import List, Dict from datetime import datetime try: import faiss import numpy as np except ImportError: faiss = None np = None class VectorMemory: """Lightweight vector memory using FAISS for semantic retrieval""" def __init__(self, dimension: int = 384, db_path: str = "zeroclaw_memory.faiss"): self.dimension = dimension self.db_path = db_path self.metadata_path = db_path.replace('.faiss', '_metadata.json') self.index = None self.metadata = [] self.load_or_create() def load_or_create(self): """Load existing FAISS index or create new one""" if faiss is None: self.index = [] self.metadata = [] return if os.path.exists(self.db_path) and os.path.exists(self.metadata_path): try: self.index = faiss.read_index(self.db_path) with open(self.metadata_path, 'r') as f: self.metadata = json.load(f) except Exception as e: print(f"Failed to load index: {e}. Creating new one.") self.index = faiss.IndexFlatL2(self.dimension) self.metadata = [] else: self.index = faiss.IndexFlatL2(self.dimension) self.metadata = [] def store_memory(self, embedding: List[float], agent_id: str, memory_type: str, content: Dict, success_score: float): """Store a memory with embedding, metadata, and success score""" if faiss is None: self.index.append({ 'embedding': embedding, 'agent_id': agent_id, 'memory_type': memory_type, 'content': content, 'success_score': success_score, 'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat() }) else: vector = np.array([embedding], dtype=np.float32) self.index.add(vector) metadata_entry = { 'agent_id': agent_id, 'memory_type': memory_type, 'content': content, 'success_score': success_score, 'timestamp': datetime.now().isoformat(), 'embedding': embedding } self.metadata.append(metadata_entry) self.save() def retrieve_memories(self, query_embedding: List[float], agent_id: str = None, k: int = 5, min_score: float = 0.5) -> List[Dict]: """Retrieve top-k similar memories for an agent""" if faiss is None: results = [] for i, mem in enumerate(self.index): if agent_id and mem['agent_id'] != agent_id: continue if mem['success_score'] >= min_score: results.append(mem) return results[:k] query_vector = np.array([query_embedding], dtype=np.float32) distances, indices = self.index.search(query_vector, min(k * 3, len(self.metadata))) results = [] for idx in indices[0]: if idx < len(self.metadata): meta = self.metadata[int(idx)] if agent_id and meta['agent_id'] != agent_id: continue if meta['success_score'] >= min_score: results.append(meta) if len(results) >= k: break return results def retrieve_by_type(self, memory_type: str, agent_id: str = None, k: int = 10) -> List[Dict]: """Retrieve all memories of a specific type""" if faiss is None: results = [m for m in self.index if m['memory_type'] == memory_type and (not agent_id or m['agent_id'] == agent_id)] str, target_agent_id: str, memory_type: str = 'mutation') -> Dict: """Twoie Logic: Target agent absorbs memories from source agent""" source_memories = self.retrieve_by_type(memory_type, agent_id=source_agent_id, k=5) absorbed_count = 0 total_score_increase = 0.0 for mem in source_memories: self.store_memory( embedding=mem.get('embedding', [0.0] * self.dimension), agent_id=target_agent_id, memory_type=f"{memory_type}_absorbed", content=mem['content'], success_score=mem['success_score'] * 0.9 ) absorbed_count += 1 total_score_increase += mem['success_score'] * 0.1 return { 'source_agent': source_agent_id, 'target_agent': target_agent_id, 'memories_absorbed': absorbed_count, 'fitness_boost': min(total_score_increase, 0.5) } def save(self): """Persist index and metadata to disk""" if faiss is not None and self.index is not None: try: faiss.write_index(self.index, self.db_path) with open(self.metadata_path, 'w') as f: json.dump(self.metadata, f, indent=2) except Exception as e: print(f"Failed to save memory: {e}") def clear(self): """Clear all memories""" if faiss is not None: self.index = faiss.IndexFlatL2(self.dimension) else: self.index = [] self.metadata = [] if os.path.exists(self.db_path): os.remove(self.db_path) if os.path.exists(self.metadata_path): os.remove(self.metadata_path) def get_agent_lineage(self, agent_id: str) -> Dict: """Get full memory lineage for an agent""" agent_memories = [m for m in self.metadata if m['agent_id'] == agent_id] return { 'agent_id': agent_id, 'total_memories': len(agent_memories), 'memory_types': list(set(m['memory_type'] for m in agent_memories)), 'avg_success_score': sum(m['success_score'] for m in agent_memories) / len(agent_memories) if agent_memories else 0.0, 'memories': GRIMOIRE_BUS.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True) ts = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S") entry = { "agent": "syntaxbuff", "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(), "task": "inference session", "insight": insight, "next_suggestion": next_suggestion, "completed": completed or [] } path = GRIMOIRE_BUS / f"syntaxbuff_{ts}.json" path.write_text(json.dumps(entry, indent=2)) return str(path) # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # PERSONA INJECTION (FORCE MULTIPLIER HOOK #4) # Loads Mikey persona seed and injects as system prompt prefix # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── def load_persona(path=PERSONA_SEED): if not path.exists(): return "" try: seed = json.loads(path.read_text()) weights = seed.get("weights", {}) style = seed.get("communication_register", "") reasoning = seed.get("reasoning_style", "") return ( f"You are operating under the Mikey persona. " f"Domain weights: {weights}. " f"Reasoning style: {reasoning}. " f"Communication: {style}. " f"Bias: {', '.join(seed.get('bias_profile', []))}. " f"Error handling: {seed.get('error_handling', '')}." ) except: return "" # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # PUTER INFERENCE # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── def call_puter(prompt, model_id, system=None): """Call Puter.js inference via Node bridge.""" bridge = HOME / "bin" / "puter_bridge.js" if not bridge.exists(): bridge = HOME / "tools" / "puter_bridge.js" if not bridge.exists(): raise RuntimeError("puter_bridge.js not found") payload = json.dumps({ "prompt": prompt, "model": model_id, "system": system or "" }) result = subprocess.run( ["node", str(bridge)], input=payload, capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=120 ) if result.returncode != 0: raise RuntimeError(f"Puter bridge error: {result.stderr[:200]}") return result.stdout.strip() def call_openrouter(prompt, model_id, system=None): """Direct OpenRouter fallback.""" key = os.environ.get("OPENROUTER_API_KEY") if not key: raise RuntimeError("OPENROUTER_API_KEY not set") headers = { "Authorization": f"Bearer {key}", "Content-Type": "application/json" } messages = [] if system: messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system}) messages.append({"role": "user", "content": prompt}) data = json.dumps({"model": model_id, "messages": messages}).encode() req = urllib.request.Request( "https://openrouter.ai/api/v1/chat/completions", data=data, headers=headers ) with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as r: return json.loads(r.read())["choices"][0]["message"]["content"] def call_groq(prompt, model_id, system=None): """Groq fallback.""" key = os.environ.get("GROQ_API_KEY") if not key: raise RuntimeError("GROQ_API_KEY not set") headers = { "Authorization": f"Bearer {key}", "Content-Type": "application/json" } messages = [] if system: messages.append({"role": "system", "content": system}) messages.append({"role": "user", "content": prompt}) data = json.dumps({"model": model_id, "messages": messages}).encode() req = urllib.request.Request( "https://api.groq.com/openai/v1/chat/completions", data=data, headers=headers ) with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=60) as r: return json.loads(r.read())["choices"][0]["message"]["content"] # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # MAIN INFERENCE DISPATCHER (FORCE MULTIPLIER HOOK #5) # Add new providers here. Model router above auto-selects. # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── def dispatch(prompt, model_key=DEFAULT_MODEL, system=None, inject_persona=False): model = MODELS.get(model_key, MODELS[DEFAULT_MODEL]) provider = model["provider"] model_id = model["id"] if inject_persona: persona = load_persona() system = f"{persona}\n\n{system}" if system else persona print(f"[syntaxbuff] model={model_key} provider={provider}", file=sys.stderr) try: if provider == "puter": return call_puter(prompt, model_id, system) elif provider == "openrouter": return call_openrouter(prompt, model_id, system) elif provider == "groq": return call_groq(prompt, model_id, system) except Exception as e: print(f"[syntaxbuff] {provider} failed: {e} — trying groq fallback", file=sys.stderr) try: return call_groq(prompt, MODELS["groq"]["id"], system) except Exception as e2: print(f"[syntaxbuff] groq also failed: {e2} — trying qwen fallback", file=sys.stderr) return call_openrouter(prompt, MODELS["qwen"]["id"], system) # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # LOG SESSION OUTPUT # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── def log_session(prompt, model_key, response): entry = { "ts": datetime.now().isoformat(), "model": model_key, "prompt_hash": hashlib.sha256(prompt.encode()).hexdigest()[:12], "response_hash": hashlib.sha256(response.encode()).hexdigest()[:12], "response_preview": response[:100] } with open(SESSION_LOG, "a") as f: f.write(json.dumps(entry) + "\n") # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # BOARDROOM SHORTCUT (FORCE MULTIPLIER HOOK #6) # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── def run_boardroom(topic): boardroom = HOME / "RootBase" / "Official-Vertical-AI-Boardroom" / "boardroom.py" if not boardroom.exists(): print("boardroom.py not found at canonical location", file=sys.stderr) return context = {"label": topic, "type": "syntaxbuff_invoke", "raw": topic} sys.path.insert(0, str(boardroom.parent)) from boardroom import run_boardroom as rb result = rb(context) print(json.dumps(result.get("synthesis", {}), indent=2)) # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # ANTI-FABRICATION WARD (auto-applied to all logged sessions) # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── WARD_HEADER = "ANTI-FABRICATION WARD ACTIVE. All results require raw source. Truth Sleuth watches." # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── # CLI ENTRYPOINT # ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────── def main(): parser = argparse.ArgumentParser( description="SyntaxBuff — Puter-powered CLI agent" ) parser.add_argument("prompt", nargs="?", help="Your prompt") parser.add_argument("--model", "-m", default=DEFAULT_MODEL, choices=list(MODELS.keys()), help="Model to use") parser.add_argument("--task", "-t", default=None, choices=list(TASK_ROUTER.keys()), help="Task type — auto-routes to best model") parser.add_argument("--persona", "-p", action="store_true", help="Inject Mikey persona seed") parser.add_argument("--recall", action="store_true", help="Show Anchor memory summary") parser.add_argument("--scry", action="store_true", help="Show latest grimoire bus entries") parser.add_argument("--models", action="store_true", help="List available models") parser.add_argument("--boardroom", action="store_true", help="Fire boardroom at prompt") parser.add_argument("--chain", default=None, help="Cast a grimoire chain by name") parser.add_argument("--system", "-s", default=None, help="System prompt override") parser.add_argument("--boot", action="store_true", help="You asked for it") args = parser.parse_args() # import sys, ast, json def parse_ast(filepath): if not os.path.exists(filepath): return {"error": f"File not found: {filepath}"} try: with open(filepath, "r") as f: tree = ast.parse(f.read()) return { "file": filepath, "classes": [node.name for node in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef)], "functions": [node.name for node in ast.walk(tree) if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef)] } except Exception as e: return {"error": str(e)} if __name__ == "__main__": if len(sys.argv) > 1: import os print(json.dumps(parse_ast(sys.argv[1]), indent=2))

""" if not os.path.exists(record_path): return f"[-] Record not found at {record_path}" with open(record_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f: content = f.read() flags_found = {} for category, terms in self.statute_matrix.items(): found_terms = [term for term in terms if term.lower() in content.lower()] if found_terms: flags_found[category] = found_terms return flags_found if __name__ == "__main__": filter_engine = PublicAccountabilityFilter() print("[*] Accountability Filter initialized. """, ( rule_num, data.get('title'), data.get('text'), data.get('violation_example'), data.get('sanction'), data.get('key_point', '') )) conn.commit() conn.close() def generate_grievance(self, state: str, attorney_name: str, bar_number: str, violation_rules: List[str], description: str, supporting_docs: List[str] = None) -> Dict: """ Generate a complete bar grievance package. Args: state: State where attorney is licensed attorney_name: Full name of attorney bar_number: Attorney's bar license number violation_rules: List of ABA rule numbers violated (e.g., ["3.3", "3.8", "8.4"]) description: Detailed description of the misconduct supporting_docs: List of supporting documents Returns: Complete grievance package ready for filing """ # Get state-specific info state_info = self.state_bar_info.get(state.lower(), self.state_bar_info.get('california')) # Generate grievance ID import hashlib import time grievance_id = f"GB-{state.upper()}-{hashlib.md5(f'{attorney_name}{time.time()}'.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]}" # Get rule details rule_details = [] for rule_num in violation_rules: rule = self.aba_rules.get(rule_num, {}) rule_details.append({ "rule": rule_num, "title": rule.get('title', 'Unknown'), "text": rule.get('text', 'Unknown') }) # Generate the grievance document grievance_document = self._generate_grievance_document( state=state, state_info=state_info, grievance_id=grievance_id, attorney_name=attorney_name, bar_number=bar_number, rule_details=rule_details, description=description, supporting_docs=supporting_docs or [] ) # Generate cover letter cover_letter = self._generate_cover_letter( state=state, state_info=state_info, grievance_id=grievance_id, attorney_name=attorney_name, violation_rules=violation_rules ) # Generate proof of service proof_of_service = self._generate_proof_of_service( state=state, grievance_id=grievance_id ) # Create filing instructions filing_instructions = self._generate_filing_instructions( state=state, state_info=state_info ) # Save to database self._save_grievance_to_db( grievance_id=grievance_id, state=state, attorney_name=attorney_name, bar_number=bar_number, violation_rules=violation_rules, description=description, status='generated' ) return { "grievance_id": grievance_id, "attorney_name": attorney_name, "bar_number": bar_number, "state": state, "violation_rules": violation_rules, "date_generated": datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d'), "documents": { "grievance": grievance_document, "cover_letter": cover_letter, "proof_of_service": proof_of_service }, "filing_instructions": filing_instructions, "state_info": state_info } def _generate_grievance_document(self, state, state_info, grievance_id, attorney_name, bar_number, rule_details, description, supporting_docs) -> str: """Generate the actual grievance form.""" doc = f""" CONFIDENTIAL ATTORNEY GRIEVANCE FORM {state.upper()} STATE BAR ASSOCIATION --- FOR OFFICIAL USE ONLY --- Grievance ID: {grievance_id} Date Filed: [TO BE FILLED BY BAR] Status: Pending Review ---------------------------------------- COMPLAINANT INFORMATION ---------------------------------------- Full Name: [YOUR NAME] Address: [YOUR ADDRESS] City: [YOUR CITY] State: [YOUR STATE] ZIP: [YOUR ZIP] Phone: [YOUR PHONE] Email: [YOUR EMAIL] Are you a current or former client of this attorney? [ ] Yes [ ] No Are you an attorney? [ ] Yes [ ] No If yes, your bar number: _______________ ---------------------------------------- ATTORNEY INFORMATION ---------------------------------------- Attorney Name: {attorney_name} Bar Number: {bar_number} Law Firm: [IF KNOWN] Firm Address: [IF KNOWN] Firm Phone: [IF KNOWN] ---------------------------------------- ALLEGATIONS OF MISCONDUCT ---------------------------------------- I, the complainant, allege that the above-named attorney has violated the following {state.upper()} Rules of Professional Conduct: """ for i, rule in enumerate(rule_details, 1): doc += f"\n{i}. Rule {rule['rule']}: {rule['title']}" doc += f"\n Text: {rule['text']}" doc += "\n" doc += f""" ---------------------------------------- DETAILED DESCRIPTION ---------------------------------------- {description} ---------------------------------------- SUPPORTING DOCUMENTS ---------------------------------------- """ if supporting_docs: for i, doc_name in enumerate(supporting_docs, 1): doc += f"{i}. {doc_name}\n" else: doc += "None submitted with this grievance.\n" doc += """ ---------------------------------------- SIGNATURE ---------------------------------------- I declare under penalty of perjury under the laws of the State of """ + state + """ that the foregoing is true and correct to the best of my knowledge and belief. Signature: ________________________________ Date: _______________ Printed Name: _____________________________ ---------------------------------------- INSTRUCTIONS FOR BAR ASSOCIATION ---------------------------------------- This grievance alleges serious violations of the Rules of Professional Conduct that warrant immediate investigation. The complainant requests: 1. Expedited review due to the serious nature of the alleged violations 2. Full investigation including review of all relevant documents and witnesses 3. Appropriate disciplinary action if violations are found 4. Notification to the complainant of the investigation's progress and outcome The complainant is aware that false allegations may result in disciplinary action against the complainant. --- Submitted to: {state_info['name']} Website: {state_info['website']} Phone: {state_info.get('phone', 'N/A')} """ return doc def _generate_cover_letter(self, state, state_info, grievance_id, attorney_name, violation_rules) -> str: """Generate cover letter for filing.""" today = datetime.now().strftime('%B %d, %Y') return f""" [YOUR NAME] [YOUR ADDRESS] [YOUR CITY, STATE, ZIP] [YOUR PHONE] [YOUR EMAIL] {today} {state_info['name']} {state_info.get('address', state_info['website'])} Re: Attorney Grievance - {attorney_name} - Grievance ID: {grievance_id} To the Honorable Members of the {state.upper()} State Bar: I am filing this formal grievance against attorney {attorney_name} for violations of the {state.upper()} Rules of Professional Conduct, specifically: {', '.join(f'Rule {r}' for r in violation_rules)} These violations constitute serious misconduct that reflects adversely on the attorney's honesty, trustworthiness, and fitness to practice law. The attached grievance form provides detailed information about the alleged violations. I request that this matter be given immediate attention and that a thorough investigation be conducted. Given the serious nature of the alleged violations, I respectfully request expedited processing. I have attached the following: - Completed grievance form - Supporting documentation (if any) - Proof of service (to be completed after filing) Please confirm receipt of this grievance and provide me with a case number for tracking purposes. I would appreciate regular updates on the status of this matter. Thank you for your attention to this important matter. Sincerely, [YOUR NAME] Enclosure(s) """ def _generate_proof_of_service(self, state, grievance_id) -> str: """Generate proof of service form.""" return f""" PROOF OF SERVICE I, [YOUR NAME], being first duly sworn upon oath, depose and state: 1. I am over the age of 18 and competent to make this declaration. 2. On [DATE OF FILING], I served the attached grievance (Grievance ID: {grievance_id}) upon the {state.upper()} State Bar by [method of service - certified mail, hand delivery, etc.]. 3. If by mail, the certified mail number(s): _______________________________ Date mailed: _______________ 6. If by hand delivery, the name of person served: _________________________ Date and time served: _______________ I declare under penalty of perjury under the laws of the State of {state} that the foregoing is true and correct. Executed on _______________ ______________________________ [YOUR NAME] Subscribed and sworn to before me on _______________ ______________________________ Notary Public / Authorized Officer My Commission Expires: _______________ """ def _generate_filing_instructions(self, state, state_info) -> List[str]: """Generate step-by-step filing instructions.""" return [ f"1. Make 3 copies of your complete grievance package (original + 2 copies)", f"2. Sign and date all documents in the presence of a notary", f"3. Mail or deliver to: {state_info['name']}", f" Address: {state_info.get('address', state_info['website'])}", f"4. If mailing, use CERTIFIED MAIL with RETURN RECEIPT REQUESTED", f"5. For serious violations, request EXPEDITED processing", f"9. Follow up if you don't receive confirmation within 30 days", f"10. File your NEXT grievance 3-4 weeks after this one (3-grievance strategy)" ] def _save_grievance_to_db(self, grievance_id, state, attorney_name, bar_number, violation_rules, description, status): """Save grievance to database.""" conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(""" INSERT INTO bar_grievances ( grievance_id, attorney_name, attorney_bar_number, state, filing_date, violation_rules, description, status ) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) """, ( grievance_id, attorney_name, bar_number, state, datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d'), json.dumps(violation_rules), description, status )) conn.commit() conn.close() def create_three_grievance_sequence(self, attorney_name: str, bar_number: str, state: str, violations: List[List[str]], descriptions: List[str]) -> Dict: """ Create a sequence of three grievances for the nuclear option. This is the KILLER STRATEGY - three grievances in sequence triggers automatic disbarment proceedings in most states. Args: attorney_name: Name of the attorney bar_number: Attorney's bar number state: State of licensure violations: List of 3 lists, each containing ABA rule numbers for that grievance descriptions: List of 3 descriptions, one for each grievance Returns: Complete sequence with timing and all documents """ if len(violations) != 3 or len(descriptions) != 3: raise ValueError("Must provide exactly 3 grievances for the nuclear sequence") sequence = { "attorney_name": attorney_name, "bar_number": bar_number, "state": state, "strategy": "Nuclear Option - Three Grievance Sequence", "expected_outcome": "Automatic disbarment proceedings", "grievances": [], "timeline": [], "warnings": [ "Once you start this sequence, the attorney WILL know you're targeting them", "Expect retaliation - be prepared", "Have ALL your evidence documented and secured", "The bar may try to dismiss - don't give up", "This will cost the attorney their career - they will fight dirty" ] } # Generate each grievance for i, (violation_set, description) in enumerate(zip(violations, descriptions), 1): grievance = self.generate_grievance( state=state, attorney_name=attorney_name, bar_number=bar_number, violation_rules=violation_set, description=description ) # Calculate timing base_days = (i - 1) * 28 # 0, 28, 56 days filing_date = datetime.now() + timedelta(days=base_days) sequence['grievances'].append({ **grievance, "sequence_number": i, "scheduled_filing_date": filing_date.strftime('%Y-%m-%d'), "days_from_start": base_days }) sequence['timeline'].append({ "day": base_days, "action": f"File Grievance #{i}", "description": f"Grievance alleging violations: {', '.join(violation_set)}" }) # Add expected timeline for disbarment sequence['timeline'].extend([ {"day": 84, "action": "Bar initiates formal investigation", "description": "Most states trigger automatic investigation after 3rd grievance"}, {"day": 90, "action": "Attorney notified of investigation", "description": "Attorney must respond within typically 14-30 days"}, {"day": 120, "action": "Insurance coverage terminated", "description": "Malpractice insurers drop coverage"}, {"day": 120, "action": "Surety bond revoked", "description": "Bond companies revoke surety"}, {"day": 150, "action": "Bar files formal charges", "description": "Formal disciplinary proceedings begin"}, {"day": 180, "action": "Disbarment hearing scheduled", "description": "Attorney cannot represent themselves"}, {"day": 210, "action": "Disbarment order issued", "description": "Attorney is officially disbarred"} ]) # Add strategy notes sequence['strategy_notes'] = { "why_three_works": "Bar associations have internal metrics. Three grievances in a short period " "triggers automatic escalation. The attorney cannot defend against all three " "simultaneously, and the pattern of misconduct becomes undeniable.", "insurance_impact": "Insurance companies have clauses that allow immediate termination if an " "attorney is under investigation for three or more grievances. This is " "independent of the bar's action.", "surety_bond_impact": "Surety bond companies have similar clauses. Without a bond, an attorney " "cannot practice law. Without insurance, they cannot afford to practice.", "self_representation_prohibition": "Disbarred attorneys CANNOT represent themselves in any court or " "tribunal. """ [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: bleak_playbook.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:13:30Z [/DNA_TAG] """ import os import re import json import sys class RealLegalAnalyzer: def __init__(self): # The actual target statutory and administrative patterns to scan for in real documents self.target_patterns = { "civil_rights_1983": [r"1983", r"color\s+of\s+law", r"deprivation\s+of\s+rights"], "criminal_conspiracy_241": [r"241", r"conspiracy\s+against\s+rights"], "criminal_deprivation_242": [r"242", r"deprivation\s+under\s+color"], "procedural_defenses": [r"immunity", r"good\s+faith", r"discretionary"], "administrative_breaches": [r"oath", r"ministerial", r"mandate", r"jurisdiction"] } def process_case_document(self, file_path): """ Reads a real raw text file, executes precise regex matching, and extracts the exact surrounding text for verification. """ if not os.path.exists(file_path): print(f"[-] ERROR: File not found at {file_path}") return None print(f"[*] Parsing real-world text from: {file_path}") with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f: lines = f.readlines() analysis_report = { "source_document": file_path, "total_lines_scanned": len(lines), "matches_found": {} } # Scan line by line to pull the actual context where matches occur for line_idx, line in enumerate(lines): clean_line = line.strip() for category, regex_list in self.target_patterns.items(): for pattern in regex_list: if re.search(pattern, clean_line, re.IGNORECASE): if category not in analysis_report["matches_found"]: analysis_report["matches_found"][category] = [] # Capture the line number and the exact text line where it happened analysis_report["matches_found"][category].append({ "line_number": line_idx + 1, "matched_pattern": pattern, "context": clean_line }) return analysis_report if __name__ == "__main__": if len(sys.argv) < 2: print("[-] Usage: bleakplay <path_to_raw_text_file>") sys.exit(1) target_file = sys.argv[1] analyzer = RealLegalAnalyzer() report = analyzer.process_case_document(target_file) if report: # Write the actual results out to a real JSON file for validation output_path = "/storage/emulated/0/RootBase/OutClaw/case_analysis_output.json" with open(output_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as out_f: json.dump(report, out_f, indent=4) print(f"[+] Scan execution complete. Real matches logged to: {output_path}") print(f"[*] Summary of Categories Found: {list(report['matches_found'].keys())}")

""" [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: bundle_splitter.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:13:30Z [/DNA_TAG] """ import os import sys from pypdf import PdfReader, PdfWriter def split_legal_bundle(input_pdf_path, chunk_size=10): if not os.path.exists(input_pdf_path): print(f"[-] Missing source file at: {input_pdf_path}") return output_dir = "/storage/emulated/0/RootBase/OutClaw/StagedChunks" os.makedirs(output_dir, exist_ok=True) print(f"[*] Opening master bundle: {input_pdf_path}") reader = PdfReader(input_pdf_path) total_pages = len(reader) print(f"[+] Total pages detected: {total_pages}") chunk_count = 0 for start_page in range(0, total_pages, chunk_size): end_page = min(start_page + chunk_size, total_pages) writer = PdfWriter() for page_num in range(start_page, end_page): writer.add_page(reader.pages[page_num]) chunk_filename = f"bundle_pages_{start_page + 1}_to_{end_page}.pdf" chunk_filepath = os.path.join(output_dir, chunk_filename) with open(chunk_filepath, "wb") as f: writer.write(f) chunk_count += 1 print(f" -> Exported Chunk {chunk_count}: Pages {start_page + 1}-{end_page}") print(f"\n[+] Splitting complete! {chunk_count} segments stored in: {output_dir}") if __name__ == "__main__": if len(sys.argv) < 2: print("[-] Usage: python bundle_splitter.py <path_to_giant_bundle.pdf>") else: split_legal_bundle(sys.argv[1])

""" [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: case_db_core.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:13:30Z [/DNA_TAG] """ import sqlite3 import os import sys import re DB_PATH = "/storage/emulated/0/RootBase/OutClaw/case_matrix.db" class CaseDatabase: def __init__(self): self.conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) self.cursor = self.conn.cursor() self._init_tables() def _init_tables(self): # Table 1: Document Metadata self.cursor.execute(""" CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS documents ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, doc_name TEXT UNIQUE, file_path TEXT, uploaded_at TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ) """) # Table 2: Raw Verified Lines (The Immutable Baseline) self.cursor.execute(""" CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS text_records ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, doc_id INTEGER, line_number INTEGER, raw_text TEXT, FOREIGN KEY(doc_id) REFERENCES documents(id) ) """) self.conn.commit() def ingest_document(self, file_path, doc_name): if not os.path.exists(file_path): print(f"[-] Document missing at path: {file_path}") return False try: self.cursor.execute("INSERT OR IGNORE INTO documents (doc_name, file_path) VALUES (?, ?)", (doc_name, file_path)) self.conn.commit() self.cursor.execute("SELECT id FROM documents WHERE doc_name = ?", (doc_name,)) doc_id = self.cursor.fetchone()[0] # Wipe existing line records for this doc to prevent duplicate scaling self.cursor.execute("DELETE FROM text_records WHERE doc_id = ?", (doc_id,)) with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8', errors='ignore') as f: lines = f.readlines() insert_buffer = [] for idx, line in enumerate(lines): insert_buffer.append((doc_id, idx + 1, line.strip())) self.cursor.executemany("INSERT INTO text_records (doc_id, line_number, raw_text) VALUES (?, ?, ?)", insert_buffer) self.conn.commit() print(f"[+] Ingested {len(lines)} un-alterable records into database for: {doc_name}") return True except Exception as e: print(f"[-] Database ingestion failure: {e}") return False def query_exact_text(self, keyword): """Returns direct, un-editable source data matches to pin down AI drift.""" self.cursor.execute(""" SELECT d.doc_name, r.line_number, r.raw_text FROM text_records r JOIN documents d ON r.doc_id = d.id WHERE r.raw_text LIKE ? """, (f"%{keyword}%",)) return self.cursor.fetchall() if __name__ == "__main__": db = CaseDatabase() print(f"[*] Local Case Matrix Database active at: {DB_PATH}")

["Individual right to own firearms", "Right to carry arms", "Shall NOT be infringed"]}, "3": {"title": "Quartering of Soldiers", "rights": ["No quartering in peacetime without consent", "Wartime only as prescribed by law"]}, "4": {"title": "Search and Seizure", "rights": ["Secure in person, house, papers, effects", "No unreasonable searches or seizures", "Warrants require: probable cause + oath + 15)", "Organize Militia (Cl. 16)", "DC Jurisdiction (Cl. 17)", "Necessary and Proper (Cl. 18) - ONLY to execute other enumerated powers" ], "framework": [ {"step": 1, "action": "Identify specific " "It only allows laws necessary and proper to EXECUTE enumerated powers. " "Cannot be used to expand federal power beyond enumeration." } def _init_db(self): """Initialize database tables.""" conn = sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(""" CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS constitutional_core ( id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, principle_id TEXT UNIQUE, title TEXT, category TEXT, text TEXT, meaning TEXT, application TEXT, warnings TEXT ) """) conn.commit() conn.close() def get_principle(self, principle_id: str) -> Optional[Dict]: """Get a constitutional principle by ID.""" principles = { "supremacy_clause": self.supremacy_clause, "tenth_amendment": self.tenth_amendment, "clearfield_doctrine": self.clearfield_doctrine, "congressional_scoping": self.congressional_scoping, "natural_law": self.natural_law, **{f"amendment_{n}": v for n, v in self.bill_of_rights.items()} } return principles.get(principle_id) def get_all_principles(self) -> Dict: """Get all constitutional principles.""" return { "supremacy_clause": self.supremacy_clause, "tenth_amendment": self.tenth_amendment, "clearfield_doctrine": self.clearfield_doctrine, "congressional_scoping": self.congressional_scoping, "natural_law": self.natural_law, "bill_of_rights": self.bill_of_rights } def analyze_law(self, law_description: str) -> Dict: """Analyze whether a law is constitutional.""" # Simplified analysis analysis = { "description": law_description, "checks": { "enumerated_power": { "question": "Does the law fall within an enumerated power?", "check": self._check_enumerated_power(law_description), "result": None }, "bill_of_rights": { "question": "Does the law violate any Bill of Rights provision?", "check": self._check_bill_of_rights(law_description), "result": None }, "tenth_amendment": { "question": "If federal, is it within delegated powers?", "check": "Requires full analysis", "result": None } }, "assessment": "Requires detailed legal analysis" } # Simple checks power_check = analysis['checks']['enumerated_power']['check'] rights_check = analysis['checks']['bill_of_rights']['check'] if not power_check['within_scope']: analysis['assessment'] = "UNCONSTITUTIONAL - Exceeds enumerated powers" elif rights_check['potential_violations']: analysis['assessment'] = "POTENTIAL RIGHTS VIOLATION - Needs review" else: analysis['assessment'] = "POSSIBLY CONSTITUTIONAL - Needs verification" return analysis def _check_enumerated_power(self, law_description: str) -> Dict: """Check if law falls within enumerated powers.""" law_lower = law_description.lower() # Identify potential power if any(w in law_lower for w in ['tax', 'spend', 'revenue']): power = "Taxation and Spending (Art. I, Sec. 8, Cl. 1)" elif any(w in law_lower for w in ['commerce', 'interstate', 'trade']): power = "Commerce (Art. I, Sec. 8, Cl. 3)" elif any(w in law_lower for w in ['military', 'army', 'navy', 'defense']): power = "Defense Powers (Art. I, Sec. 8, Cl. 11-16)" elif any(w in law_lower for w in ['money', 'coin', 'currency']): power = "Coin Money (Art. I, Sec. 8, Cl. 5)" elif any(w in law_lower for w in ['post', 'mail']): power = "Post Offices (Art. I, Sec. 8, Cl. 7)" else: power = "UNKNOWN - May exceed enumerated powers" return { "potential_power": power, "within_scope": power != "UNKNOWN - May exceed enumerated powers", "note": "This is a preliminary check only. Full legal analysis required." } def _check_bill_of_rights(self, law_description: str) -> Dict: """Check for potential Bill of Rights violations.""" violations = [] law_lower = law_description.lower() if any(w in law_lower for w in ['speech', 'press', 'censor', 'restrict']): violations.append({"amendment": 1, "right": "Freedom of Speech/Press"}) if any(w in law_lower for w in ['arms', 'gun', 'firearm', 'weapon']): violations.append({"amendment": 2, "right": "Right to Keep and Bear Arms"}) if any(w in law_lower for w in ['search', 'seizure', 'warrantless', 'surveillance']): violations.append({"amendment": 4, "right": "Protection from Unreasonable Search and Seizure"}) if any(w in law_lower for w in ['due process', 'life liberty property']): violations.append({"amendment": 5, "right": "Due Process of Law"}) if any(w in law_lower for w in ['self-incriminat', 'remain silent']): violations.append({"amendment": 5, "right": "Protection from Self-Incrimination"}) return { "potential_violations": violations, "violation_found": len(violations) > 0, "note": "Preliminary check. Full legal analysis required." } def search(self, query: str) -> List[Dict]: """Search constitutional principles.""" results = [] query_lower = query.lower() # Search supremacy clause if query_lower in json.dumps(self.supremacy_clause).lower(): results.append({"id": "supremacy_clause", "data": self.supremacy_clause}) # Search tenth amendment if query_lower in json.dumps(self.tenth_amendment).lower(): results.append({"id": "tenth_amendment", "data": self.tenth_amendment}) # Search clearfield if query_lower in json.dumps(self.clearfield_doctrine).lower(): results.append({"id": "clearfield_doctrine", "data": self.clearfield_doctrine}) # Search congressional scoping if query_lower in json.dumps(self.congressional_scoping).lower(): results.append({"id": "congressional_scoping", "data": self.congressional_scoping}) # Search natural law if query_lower in json.dumps(self.natural_law).lower(): results.append({"id": "natural_law", "data": self.natural_law}) # Search Bill of Rights for num, amend in self.bill_of_rights.items(): if query_lower in json.dumps(amend).lower(): results.append({"id": f"amendment_{num}", "data": amend}) return results # Singleton _core = None def get_constitutional_core(): global _core if _core is None: _core = ConstitutionalCore() return _core if __name__ == "__main__": print("=" * 80) print("OUTCLAW CONSTITUTIONAL CORE") print("The Laws of Nature and Nature's God are Supreme") print("=" * 80) core = get_constitutional_core() # Show Supremacy Clause print("\n[SUPREMACY CLAUSE - """ [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: event_bus.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:13:30Z [/DNA_TAG] """ #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ OUTCLAW EVENT BUS - Inter-Process Communication System This module provides a lightweight event bus for coordinating between multiple AI agents (Mistral Vibe, Gemini CLI, etc.) working on the same project. Prevents file conflicts, duplicates work, and enables parallel processing. """ import json import os import time import threading import hashlib from datetime import datetime from pathlib import Path # Central coordination directory BUS_DIR = "/storage/emulated/0/RootBase/OutClaw/.event_bus" Path(BUS_DIR).mkdir(exist_ok=True) class EventBus: """ Distributed event bus for multi-agent coordination. Uses file-based messaging for cross-process communication. """ def __init__(self, agent_name="unknown"): self.agent_name = agent_name self.lock_file = os.path.join(BUS_DIR, "bus.lock") self.event_log = os.path.join(BUS_DIR, "event_log.json") self.task_registry = os.path.join(BUS_DIR, "task_registry.json") self.agent_status = os.path.join(BUS_DIR, "agent_status.json") # Initialize if not exists self._initialize() def _initialize(self): """Initialize bus files if they don't exist.""" if not os.path.exists(self.event_log): with open(self.event_log, 'w') as f: json.dump([], f) if not os.path.exists(self.task_registry): with open(self.task_registry, 'w') as f: json.dump({}, f) if not os.path.exists(self.agent_status): with open(self.agent_status, 'w') as f: json.dump({}, f) def _acquire_lock(self, timeout=10): """Acquire file lock for thread-safe operations.""" start_time = time.time() while time.time() - start_time < timeout: try: fd = os.open(self.lock_file, os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY) os.close(fd) return True except FileExistsError: time.sleep(0.1) return False def _release_lock(self): """Release file lock.""" if os.path.exists(self.lock_file): os.unlink(self.lock_file) def _read_json(self, filepath): """Read JSON file with lock.""" if not os.path.exists(filepath): return {} with open(filepath, 'r') as f: return json.load(f) def _write_json(self, filepath, data): """Write JSON file with lock.""" with open(filepath, 'w') as f: json.dump(data, f, indent=2) def register_agent(self, capabilities=None): """Register this agent's capabilities.""" status = self._read_json(self.agent_status) status[self.agent_name] = { "last_seen": datetime.now().isoformat(), "capabilities": capabilities or [], "status": "active" } self._write_json(self.agent_status, status) def emit_event(self, event_type, data, priority="normal"): """Emit an event to the bus.""" event = { "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(), "agent": self.agent_name, "type": event_type, "data": data, "priority": priority } log = self._read_json(self.event_log) if isinstance(log, list): log.append(event) else: log = [event] self._write_json(self.event_log, log) return event def subscribe(self, event_type, callback): """Subscribe to events (polling-based).""" def poller(): last_index = 0 while True: log = self._read_json(self.event_log) if isinstance(log, list): for i, event in enumerate(log[last_index:], last_index): if event.get('type') == event_type: callback(event) last_index = len(log) time.sleep(1) thread = threading.Thread(target=poller, daemon=True) thread.start() return thread def get_unread_events(self, agent_name=None, event_type=None): """Get unread events for this agent.""" log = self._read_json(self.event_log) events = [] for event in (log or []): if agent_name and event.get('agent') == agent_name: continue # Skip our own events if event_type and event.get('type') != event_type: continue events.append(event) return events def claim_task(self, task_name, description, agent=None): """ Claim a task for processing. Returns True if task was claimed, False if already claimed by another agent. """ registry = self._read_json(self.task_registry) if task_name in registry: # Task already exists existing = registry[task_name] if existing.get('status') == 'completed': return False, "Task already completed" if existing.get('assigned_to') != self.agent_name: return False, f"Task assigned to {existing.get('assigned_to')}" return True, "Task already claimed by you" # Claim the task registry[task_name] = { "description": description, "assigned_to": agent or self.agent_name, "claimed_at": datetime.now().isoformat(), "status": "in_progress", "progress": 0 } self._write_json(self.task_registry, registry) return True, "Task claimed" def update_task_progress(self, task_name, progress, status=None, result=None): """Update task progress.""" registry = self._read_json(self.task_registry) if task_name not in registry: return False, "Task not found" task = registry[task_name] task['progress'] = progress if status: task['status'] = status if result: task['result'] = result task['last_updated'] = datetime.now().isoformat() self._write_json(self.task_registry, registry) return True, "Task updated" def complete_task(self, task_name, result): """Mark task as completed.""" success, msg = self.update_task_progress( task_name, progress=100, status="completed", result=result ) if success: # Emit completion event self.emit_event("task_completed", { "task": task_name, "result": result, "agent": self.agent_name }) return success, msg def get_task_status(self, task_name): """Get status of a task.""" registry = self._read_json(self.task_registry) return registry.get(task_name, None) def list_pending_tasks(self): """List all pending tasks.""" registry = self._read_json(self.task_registry) return { k: v for k, v in registry.items() if v.get('status') != 'completed' } def get_agent_capabilities(self, agent_name): """Get capabilities of a specific agent.""" status = self._read_json(self.agent_status) return status.get(agent_name, {}).get('capabilities', []) def list_active_agents(self): """List all active agents.""" status = self._read_json(self.agent_status) # Filter out agents not seen in last 5 minutes cutoff = datetime.now().timestamp() - 300 active = {} for name, info in status.items(): last_seen = datetime.fromisoformat(info.get('last_seen', '1970-01-01')).timestamp() if last_seen > cutoff: active[name] = info return active def file_lock(self, filepath, operation, timeout=30): """ Acquire lock for a specific file operation. Prevents concurrent modification conflicts. Usage: with bus.file_lock('/path/to/file.txt', 'write'): # Safe to modify file """ lock_path = os.path.join(BUS_DIR, "file_locks", hashlib.md5(filepath.encode()).hexdigest() + ".lock") Path(lock_path).parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True) class FileLockContext: def __enter__(self): start = time.time() while time.time() - start < timeout: try: fd = os.open(lock_path, os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL | os.O_WRONLY) os.close(fd) return self except FileExistsError: time.sleep(0.5) raise TimeoutError(f"Could not acquire lock for {filepath}") def __exit__(self, *args): if os.path.exists(lock_path): os.unlink(lock_path) return FileLockContext() # Global event bus instance _bus_instance = None def get_bus(agent_name="mistral_vibe"): """Get or create the global event bus instance.""" global _bus_instance if _bus_instance is None: _bus_instance = EventBus(agent_name) _bus_instance.register_agent() return _bus_instance # Convenience functions for direct use def emit(event_type, data, priority="normal", agent="mistral_vibe"): """Quick emit function.""" bus = get_bus(agent) return bus.emit_event(event_type, data, priority) def claim(task_name, description): """Quick task claim function.""" bus = get_bus() return bus.claim_task(task_name, description) def complete(task_name, result): """Quick task complete function.""" bus = get_bus() return bus.complete_task(task_name, result) def status(task_name): """Quick task status check.""" bus = get_bus() return bus.get_task_status(task_name) def pending_tasks(): """Get pending tasks.""" bus = get_bus() return bus.list_pending_tasks() def other_agents(): """List other active agents.""" bus = get_bus() agents = bus.list_active_agents() return {k: v for k, v in agents.items() if k != bus.agent_name} if __name__ == "__main__": # Test the event bus bus = get_bus("test_agent") # Register capabilities bus.register_agent(capabilities=["document_analysis", "template_generation", "legal_research"]) # Claim a task success, msg = bus.claim_task("test_task", "Testing the event bus system") print(f"Task claim: {success} - {msg}") # Update progress bus.update_task_progress("test_task", progress=50) # Complete task bus.complete_task("test_task", result={"status": "success", "data": "test complete"}) # List pending print(f"\nPending tasks: {bus.list_pending_tasks()}") # Get task status print(f"\nTask status: {bus.get_task_status('test_task')}") # Emit event bus.emit_event("system_test", {"message": "Event bus operational"}) print("\n[+] Event Bus System Operational")
