""" # Singleton instance _instance = None def __init__(self, db_path: str = DB_PATH): self.db_path = db_path self._init_db() @classmethod def get_engine(cls) -> 'FamilyAdvocacyEngine': """Get singleton instance of the engine.""" if cls._instance is None: cls._instance = cls() return cls._instance def _init_db(self) -> None: """Initialize database tables for family advocacy.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() # Victims table cursor.execute(''' CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS advocacy_victims ( victim_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL, date_of_birth TEXT, current_location TEXT, victim_category TEXT NOT NULL, incident_date TEXT, incident_description TEXT NOT NULL, case_number TEXT, facility_name TEXT, related_documents TEXT, created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ) ''') # Family members table cursor.execute(''' CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS advocacy_family_members ( family_member_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL, relationship TEXT NOT NULL, contact_info TEXT NOT NULL, is_legal_guardian INTEGER DEFAULT 0, has_power_of_attorney INTEGER DEFAULT 0, created_at TEXT DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ) ''') # Family cases table cursor.execute(''' CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS advocacy_cases ( case_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, case_title TEXT NOT NULL, victim_id TEXT NOT NULL, jurisdiction TEXT NOT NULL, summary TEXT NOT NULL, created_date TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT DEFAULT 'ACTIVE', FOREIGN KEY (victim_id) REFERENCES advocacy_victims(victim_id) ) ''') # Case-family member linkage cursor.execute(''' CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS advocacy_case_members ( case_id TEXT NOT NULL, family_member_id TEXT NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (case_id, family_member_id), FOREIGN KEY (case_id) REFERENCES advocacy_cases(case_id), FOREIGN KEY (family_member_id) REFERENCES advocacy_family_members(family_member_id) ) ''') # Actions taken cursor.execute(''' CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS advocacy_actions ( action_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, case_id TEXT NOT NULL, action_type TEXT NOT NULL, action_date TEXT NOT NULL, description TEXT NOT NULL, result TEXT, documents TEXT, FOREIGN KEY (case_id) REFERENCES advocacy_cases(case_id) ) ''') # Evidence collected cursor.execute(''' CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS advocacy_evidence ( evidence_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, case_id TEXT NOT NULL, evidence_type TEXT NOT NULL, description TEXT NOT NULL, source TEXT NOT NULL, date_obtained TEXT NOT NULL, location TEXT, FOREIGN KEY (case_id) REFERENCES advocacy_cases(case_id) ) ''') # Contacts cursor.execute(''' CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS advocacy_contacts ( contact_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, case_id TEXT NOT NULL, name TEXT NOT NULL, title TEXT, organization TEXT NOT NULL, phone TEXT, email TEXT, notes TEXT, FOREIGN KEY (case_id) REFERENCES advocacy_cases(case_id) ) ''') conn.commit() # ========================================================================= # VICTIM MANAGEMENT # ========================================================================= def register_victim(self, victim: Victim) -> str: """Register a victim in the database.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(''' INSERT OR REPLACE INTO advocacy_victims (victim_id, name, date_of_birth, current_location, victim_category, incident_date, incident_description, case_number, facility_name, related_documents) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ''', ( victim.victim_id, victim.name, victim.date_of_birth, victim.current_location, victim.victim_category, victim.incident_date, victim.incident_description, victim.case_number, victim.facility_name, json.dumps(victim.related_documents) )) conn.commit() return victim.victim_id def get_victim(self, victim_id: str) -> Optional[Victim]: """Retrieve a victim by ID.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(''' SELECT victim_id, name, date_of_birth, current_location, victim_category, incident_date, incident_description, case_number, facility_name, related_documents FROM advocacy_victims WHERE victim_id = ? ''', (victim_id,)) row = cursor.fetchone() if row: victim = Victim( name=row[1], date_of_birth=row[2], current_location=row[3], victim_category=row[4], incident_date=row[5], incident_description=row[6], case_number=row[7], facility_name=row[8] ) victim.victim_id = row[0] victim.related_documents = json.loads(row[9]) if row[9] else [] return victim return None def list_victims(self, category: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]: """List all victims, optionally filtered by category.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() query = ''' SELECT victim_id, name, victim_category, incident_date, incident_description, current_location, facility_name FROM advocacy_victims ''' params = [] if category: query += " WHERE victim_category = ?" params.append(category) query += " ORDER BY incident_date DESC" cursor.execute(query, params) return [ { "victim_id": row[0], "name": row[1], "category": row[2], "incident_date": row[3], "description": row[4], "location": row[5], "facility": row[6] } for row in cursor.fetchall() ] # ========================================================================= # FAMILY MEMBER MANAGEMENT # ========================================================================= def register_family_member(self, member: FamilyMember) -> str: """Register a family member in the database.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(''' INSERT OR REPLACE INTO advocacy_family_members (family_member_id, name, relationship, contact_info, is_legal_guardian, has_power_of_attorney) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ''', ( member.family_member_id, member.name, member.relationship, json.dumps(member.contact_info), int(member.is_legal_guardian), int(member.has_power_of_attorney) )) conn.commit() return member.family_member_id def get_family_member(self, member_id: str) -> Optional[FamilyMember]: """Retrieve a family member by ID.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(''' SELECT family_member_id, name, relationship, contact_info, is_legal_guardian, has_power_of_attorney FROM advocacy_family_members WHERE family_member_id = ? ''', (member_id,)) row = cursor.fetchone() if row: member = FamilyMember( name=row[1], relationship=row[2], contact_info=json.loads(row[3]), is_legal_guardian=bool(row[4]), has_power_of_attorney=bool(row[5]) ) member.family_member_id = row[0] return member return None def list_family_members(self) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]: """List all family members.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(''' SELECT family_member_id, name, relationship, is_legal_guardian, has_power_of_attorney FROM advocacy_family_members ORDER BY name ''') return [ { "family_member_id": row[0], "name": row[1], "relationship": row[2], "has_legal_standing": bool(row[3] or row[4]) } for row in cursor.fetchall() ] # ========================================================================= # CASE MANAGEMENT # ========================================================================= def create_case(self, case_title: str, family_members: List[FamilyMember], victim: Victim, jurisdiction: str, summary: str) -> FamilyAdvocacyCase: """ Create a new family advocacy case. Args: case_title: Short title for the case family_members: List of FamilyMember objects advocating victim: The Victim being advocated for jurisdiction: Jurisdiction (state, county, etc.) summary: Brief description of the case Returns: New FamilyAdvocacyCase object """ case_obj = FamilyAdvocacyCase( case_title=case_title, family_members=family_members, victim=victim, jurisdiction=jurisdiction, summary=summary ) # Save to database self._save_case(case_obj) return case_obj def _save_case(self, case_obj: FamilyAdvocacyCase) -> None: """Save a case to the database.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() # Save victim self.register_victim(case_obj.victim) # Save family members for member in case_obj.family_members: self.register_family_member(member) # Save case cursor.execute(''' INSERT OR REPLACE INTO advocacy_cases (case_id, case_title, victim_id, jurisdiction, summary, created_date, status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ''', ( case_obj.case_id, case_obj.case_title, case_obj.victim.victim_id, case_obj.jurisdiction, case_obj.summary, case_obj.created_date, case_obj.status )) # Link family members to case for member in case_obj.family_members: cursor.execute(''' INSERT OR REPLACE INTO advocacy_case_members (case_id, family_member_id) VALUES (?, ?) ''', (case_obj.case_id, member.family_member_id)) # Save actions for action in case_obj.actions_taken: cursor.execute(''' INSERT OR REPLACE INTO advocacy_actions (action_id, case_id, action_type, action_date, description, result, documents) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ''', ( action["action_id"], case_obj.case_id, action["action_type"], action["action_date"], action["description"], action.get("result"), json.dumps(action.get("documents", [])) )) # Save evidence for evidence in case_obj.evidence_collected: cursor.execute(''' INSERT OR REPLACE INTO advocacy_evidence (evidence_id, case_id, evidence_type, description, source, date_obtained, location) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ''', ( evidence["evidence_id"], case_obj.case_id, evidence["evidence_type"], evidence["description"], evidence["source"], evidence["date_obtained"], evidence.get("location") )) # Save contacts for contact in case_obj.contacts: cursor.execute(''' INSERT OR REPLACE INTO advocacy_contacts (contact_id, case_id, name, title, organization, phone, email, notes) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?) ''', ( contact["contact_id"], case_obj.case_id, contact["name"], contact.get("title"), contact["organization"], contact.get("phone"), contact.get("email"), contact.get("notes", "") )) conn.commit() def get_case(self, case_id: str) -> Optional[FamilyAdvocacyCase]: """Retrieve a case by ID.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(''' SELECT case_id, case_title, victim_id, jurisdiction, summary, created_date, status FROM advocacy_cases WHERE case_id = ? ''', (case_id,)) row = cursor.fetchone() if not row: return None # Get victim victim = self.get_victim(row[2]) if not victim: return None # Get family members cursor.execute(''' SELECT family_member_id FROM advocacy_case_members WHERE case_id = ? ''', (case_id,)) family_members = [] for member_row in cursor.fetchall(): member = self.get_family_member(member_row[0]) if member: family_members.append(member) case_obj = FamilyAdvocacyCase( case_title=row[1], family_members=family_members, victim=victim, jurisdiction=row[3], summary=row[4] ) case_obj.case_id = row[0] case_obj.created_date = row[5] case_obj.status = row[6] # Load actions cursor.execute(''' SELECT action_id, action_type, action_date, description, result, documents FROM advocacy_actions WHERE case_id = ? ORDER BY action_date ''', (case_id,)) for action_row in cursor.fetchall(): case_obj.actions_taken.append({ "action_id": action_row[0], "action_type": action_row[1], "action_date": action_row[2], "description": action_row[3], "result": action_row[4], "documents": json.loads(action_row[5]) if action_row[5] else [] }) # Load evidence cursor.execute(''' SELECT evidence_id, evidence_type, description, source, date_obtained, location FROM advocacy_evidence WHERE case_id = ? ORDER BY date_obtained ''', (case_id,)) for evidence_row in cursor.fetchall(): case_obj.evidence_collected.append({ "evidence_id": evidence_row[0], "evidence_type": evidence_row[1], "description": evidence_row[2], "source": evidence_row[3], "date_obtained": evidence_row[4], "location": evidence_row[5] }) # Load contacts cursor.execute(''' SELECT contact_id, name, title, organization, phone, email, notes FROM advocacy_contacts WHERE case_id = ? ORDER BY name ''', (case_id,)) for contact_row in cursor.fetchall(): case_obj.contacts.append({ "contact_id": contact_row[0], "name": contact_row[1], "title": contact_row[2], "organization": contact_row[3], "phone": contact_row[4], "email": contact_row[5], "notes": contact_row[6] }) return case_obj def list_cases(self, status: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]: """List all cases, optionally filtered by status.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() query = ''' SELECT c.case_id, c.case_title, c.jurisdiction, c.created_date, c.status, v.name, v.victim_category FROM advocacy_cases c JOIN advocacy_victims v ON c.victim_id = v.victim_id ''' params = [] if status: query += " WHERE c.status = ?" params.append(status) query += " ORDER BY c.created_date DESC" cursor.execute(query, params) return [ { "case_id": row[0], "case_title": row[1], "jurisdiction": row[2], "created_date": row[3], "status": row[4], "victim_name": row[5], "victim_category": row[6] } for row in cursor.fetchall() ] def close_case(self, case_id: str, resolution: str) -> bool: """Close a case with a resolution.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(''' UPDATE advocacy_cases SET status = ?, summary = ? WHERE case_id = ? ''', ("CLOSED", f"{resolution} - Closed {datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%m-%d')}", case_id)) conn.commit() return cursor.rowcount > 0 # ========================================================================= # DOCUMENT GENERATION # ========================================================================= def generate_habeas_corpus_petition(self, case_obj: FamilyAdvocacyCase, court_name: str) -> str: """ Generate a Habeas Corpus petition for wrongful incarceration. Habeas Corpus ("produce the body") is the fundamental right to challenge unlawful detention. """ primary = case_obj.get_primary_advocate() if not primary: return "ERROR: No family member with legal standing found" today = datetime.now().strftime("%B %d, %Y") document = f"""PETITION FOR WRIT OF HABEAS CORPUS IN THE [DISTRICT/SUPERIOR] COURT OF {court_name} {primary.name}, Petitioner, vs. [WARDEN/JAILER NAME], in their official capacity as [TITLE], and [AGENCY NAME], Respondent. ) CASE NO.: [TO BE ASSIGNED] PETITION FOR WRIT OF HABEAS CORPUS TO THE HONORABLE COURT: Comes now {primary.name}, {primary.relationship} of {case_obj.victim.name}, and respectfully petitions this Honorable Court for a Writ of Habeas Corpus on behalf of {case_obj.victim.name}, and in support states as follows: 1. PETITIONER'S INTEREST Petitioner is the {primary.relationship} of {case_obj.victim.name}. {primary.get_standing_description()}. Petitioner has a direct and substantial interest in the welfare and freedom of {case_obj.victim.name}. 2. DETENTION INFORMATION {case_obj.victim.name} is currently detained at: {case_obj.victim.current_location} Facility: {case_obj.victim.facility_name or 'Unknown'} Case/Booking Number: {case_obj.victim.case_number or 'Unknown'} 3. GROUNDS FOR HABEAS CORPUS The detention of {case_obj.victim.name} is unlawful because: {case_obj.victim.incident_description} 4. EXHAUSTION OF REMEDIES [List any appeals, grievances, or other remedies that have been pursued] OR No adequate remedy exists at law because [EXPLAIN] 5. REQUEST FOR RELIEF WHEREFORE, Petitioner respectfully requests that this Honorable Court: 1. Issue a Writ of Habeas Corpus directing Respondent to produce {case_obj.victim.name} before this Court; 2. Order the immediate release of {case_obj.victim.name} from unlawful detention; 3. Declare the detention of {case_obj.victim.name} to be unlawful and in violation of [SPECIFIC LAWS]; 4. Award such other and further relief as the Court deems just and proper. I declare under penalty of perjury that the foregoing is true and correct to the best of my knowledge and belief. Respectfully submitted, DATED: {today} _________________________ {primary.name} Petitioner VERIFICATION I, {primary.name}, declare under penalty of perjury that I am the {primary.relationship} of {case_obj.victim.name}, that I have personal knowledge of the facts stated herein, and that the foregoing is true and correct. DATED: {today} _________________________ {primary.name} """ return document def generate_next_friend_complaint(self, case_obj: FamilyAdvocacyCase, defendant: str, defendant_title: str) -> str: """ Generate a Next Friend complaint. A "next friend" is someone who acts on behalf of another who cannot act for themselves (incapacitated, minor, or otherwise unable). """ primary = case_obj.get_primary_advocate() if not primary: return "ERROR: No family member with legal standing found" today = datetime.now().strftime("%B %d, %Y") category_info = case_obj.victim.get_category_details() document = f"""COMPLAINT - NEXT FRIEND ACTION IN THE [DISTRICT] COURT OF {case_obj.jurisdiction} {primary.name}, as Next Friend of {case_obj.victim.name}, Plaintiff, vs. {defendant}, {defendant_title}, Defendant. ) CASE NO.: [TO BE ASSIGNED] COMPLAINT FOR [VIOLATION OF RIGHTS] JURISDICTION AND VENUE 1. This Court has jurisdiction over this matter pursuant to [STATUTE]. 2. Venue is proper in this district because the events giving rise to this action occurred in {case_obj.jurisdiction}. PARTIES 3. Plaintiff {primary.name} is the {primary.relationship} of {case_obj.victim.name} and acts as Next Friend on behalf of {case_obj.victim.name} who is {case_obj.victim.get_category_details()['description'].lower()}. 4. {primary.get_standing_description()}. 5. Defendant {defendant} is [DESCRIBE DEFENDANT'S ROLE]. FACTUAL ALLEGATIONS 6. {case_obj.victim.name} (DOB: {case_obj.victim.date_of_birth}) was {case_obj.victim.get_category_details()['description'].lower()} on or about {case_obj.victim.incident_date}. 7. The incident occurred as follows: {case_obj.victim.incident_description} CAUSES OF ACTION 8. COUNT I: [PRIMARY CAUSE OF ACTION - e.g., Violation of 42 U.S.C. § 1983] Defendant, acting under color of state law, deprived {case_obj.victim.name} of rights secured by the Constitution and laws of the United States, including but not limited to: [LIST SPECIFIC RIGHTS VIOLATED] 9. COUNT II: [SECOND CAUSE OF ACTION - e.g., False Imprisonment] Defendant intentionally and unlawfully restrained {case_obj.victim.name} of their liberty without legal justification. 10. COUNT III: [THIRD CAUSE OF ACTION - e.g., Intentional Infliction of Emotional Distress] Defendant's conduct was so outrageous in character, and so extreme in degree, as to go beyond all possible bounds of decency, and to be regarded as atrocious and utterly intolerable in a civilized community. DAMAGES 11. As a direct and proximate result of Defendant's actions, {case_obj.victim.name} has suffered: a. Physical injuries: [DESCRIBE] b. Emotional distress: [DESCRIBE] c. Economic damages: [DESCRIBE] d. Damage to reputation: [DESCRIBE] e. Loss of liberty and enjoyment of life: [DESCRIBE] 12. Plaintiff {primary.name}, as Next Friend, has also suffered emotional distress and damage to familial relationships. JURY DEMAND 13. Plaintiff demands a trial by jury on all issues so triable. PRAYER FOR RELIEF WHEREFORE, Plaintiff respectfully requests that this Honorable Court: 1. Enter judgment in favor of Plaintiff and against Defendant; 2. Award compensatory damages in an amount to be proven at trial; 3. Award punitive damages in an amount sufficient to punish and deter Defendant's conduct; 4. Award injunctive relief as appropriate; 5. Award costs of suit and reasonable attorney's fees; 6. Grant such other and further relief as the Court deems just and proper. I declare under penalty of perjury that the foregoing is true and correct to the best of my knowledge and belief. Respectfully submitted, DATED: {today} _________________________ {primary.name} Next Friend of {case_obj.victim.name} """ return document def generate_public_records_request(self, case_obj: FamilyAdvocacyCase, agency: str, records_description: str) -> str: """ Generate a public records request under FOIA or state equivalent. Families have a right to access public records related to their loved ones' cases. """ primary = case_obj.get_primary_advocate() if not primary: return "ERROR: No family member with legal standing found" today = datetime.now().strftime("%B %d, %Y") document = f"""PUBLIC RECORDS REQUEST [today] {agency} [AGENCY ADDRESS] Attention: Records Custodian / FOIA Officer Re: Public Records Request - {case_obj.case_title} To Whom It May Concern: Pursuant to the [FREEDOM OF INFORMATION ACT / STATE PUBLIC RECORDS ACT], I, {primary.name}, {primary.relationship} of {case_obj.victim.name}, hereby request access to and copies of the following public records: {records_description} These records are requested in connection with the following matter: {case_obj.summary} {case_obj.victim.name} (DOB: {case_obj.victim.date_of_birth}) was {case_obj.victim.get_category_details()['description'].lower()} on {case_obj.victim.incident_date} at {case_obj.victim.current_location}. SPECIFIC RECORDS REQUESTED: 1. All incident reports related to {case_obj.victim.name} 2. All arrest reports and booking information 3. All video/audio recordings from the date of incident 4. All witness statements 5. All medical records in the possession of the agency 6. All internal investigations or reviews 7. All communications between agency personnel regarding this matter 8. All policies and procedures relevant to the incident 9. [ANY OTHER SPECIFIC RECORDS] FEES I request a fee waiver pursuant to [STATUTE] because this request is made in the public interest and not for commercial purposes. The information sought will contribute significantly to public understanding of government operations and activities. If a fee waiver is denied, please notify me of the estimated costs before processing this request. FORMAT Please provide records in electronic format if available. If electronic format is not available, please provide paper copies. RESPONSE TIME Pursuant to [STATUTE], please provide these records within the statutory timeframe of [X] business days. If you cannot comply within this timeframe, please notify me in writing of the reason for the delay and the date by which the records will be provided. CONTACT INFORMATION {primary.name} {primary.contact_info.get('address', '[ADDRESS]')} {primary.contact_info.get('city', '[CITY]')}, {primary.contact_info.get('state', '[STATE]')} {primary.contact_info.get('zip', '[ZIP]')} {primary.contact_info.get('phone', '[PHONE]')} {primary.contact_info.get('email', '[EMAIL]')} I declare under penalty of perjury that I am the person I claim to be and that this request is made in good faith. Sincerely, _________________________ {primary.name} {primary.relationship} of {case_obj.victim.name} [ENCLOSURES: Copy of ID, Proof of Relationship if required] """ return document def generate_cease_and_desist_letter(self, case_obj: FamilyAdvocacyCase, target: str, target_title: str, harmful_actions: List[str]) -> str: """ Generate a cease and desist letter for harassment or other ongoing harm. """ primary = case_obj.get_primary_advocate() if not primary: return "ERROR: No family member with legal standing found" today = datetime.now().strftime("%B %d, %Y") actions_list = "\n".join([f" {i+1}. {action}" for i, action in enumerate(harmful_actions)]) document = f"""CEASE AND DESIST LETTER [today] VIA CERTIFIED MAIL - RETURN RECEIPT REQUESTED VIA EMAIL: [EMAIL ADDRESS] {target} {target_title} [AGENCY/ORGANIZATION] [ADDRESS] Re: Cease and Desist Demand - {case_obj.case_title} {target}: I, {primary.name}, {primary.relationship} of {case_obj.victim.name}, am writing to formally demand that you IMMEDIATELY CEASE AND DESIST from all harmful, harassing, or unlawful actions directed at {case_obj.victim.name} and our family. BACKGROUND {case_obj.victim.name} (DOB: {case_obj.victim.date_of_birth}) is the victim of {case_obj.victim.get_category_details()['description'].lower()} that occurred on or about {case_obj.victim.incident_date}. The incident is described as follows: {case_obj.victim.incident_description} SPECIFIC ACTIONS TO CEASE The following actions must cease immediately: {actions_list} LEGAL BASIS Your actions violate: - [SPECIFIC STATUTES - e.g., 42 U.S.C. § 1983, 18 U.S.C. § 241, etc.] - [STATE LAWS] - Constitutional rights to [LIST RIGHTS] - [AGENCY POLICIES AND PROCEDURES] DEMAND I demand that you and all persons acting under your direction or control: 1. IMMEDIATELY CEASE all harassing, retaliatory, or harmful actions against {case_obj.victim.name} and all family members; 2. REFRAIN from any further contact with {case_obj.victim.name} or family members except through legal counsel; 3. PRESERVE all evidence related to this matter, including but not limited to records, communications, and other documentation; 4. PROVIDE written confirmation within FIVE (5) DAYS of receipt of this letter that you have complied with this demand. WARNING Be advised that if you fail to comply with this demand, I will pursue all available legal remedies, including but not limited to: - Filing criminal complaints for harassment, stalking, or other offenses - Filing civil lawsuits for damages - Seeking restraining orders and injunctive relief - Reporting your conduct to oversight bodies and licensing authorities - Public disclosure of your actions This letter serves as formal notice and creates a record of your knowledge of these demands. Your continued actions after receipt of this notice will be considered willful and intentional, subjecting you to enhanced damages and penalties. Please govern yourself accordingly. Sincerely, _________________________ {primary.name} {primary.relationship} of {case_obj.victim.name} CC: [List of other recipients] [Attorney if applicable] [Oversight bodies] """ return document def generate_media_press_release(self, case_obj: FamilyAdvocacyCase, headline: str) -> str: """ Generate a press release to bring public attention to the case. Public pressure is a powerful tool for accountability. """ primary = case_obj.get_primary_advocate() if not primary: return "ERROR: No family member with legal standing found" today = datetime.now().strftime("%B %d, %Y") category_info = case_obj.victim.get_category_details() document = f"""FOR IMMEDIATE RELEASE {headline} {case_obj.jurisdiction} - {today} FAMILY DEMANDS JUSTICE FOR {case_obj.victim.name.upper()} {case_obj.jurisdiction} - The family of {case_obj.victim.name}, a {case_obj.victim.date_of_birth} year-old [RACE/ETHNICITY IF RELEVANT] who was {case_obj.victim.get_category_details()['description'].lower()}, is speaking out demanding accountability and justice. According to family spokesperson {primary.name}, {case_obj.victim.name} was {case_obj.victim.get_category_details()['description'].lower()} on {case_obj.victim.incident_date} at {case_obj.victim.current_location}. "{case_obj.summary}" said {primary.name}. DESCRIPTION OF INCIDENT: {case_obj.victim.incident_description} FAMILY DEMANDS: The family is demanding: 1. Immediate [RELEASE/INVESTIGATION/OTHER ACTION] for {case_obj.victim.name} 2. Full transparency and accountability from all involved agencies 3. Independent investigation into the incident 4. Disciplinary action against those responsible 5. Compensation for the harm caused to {case_obj.victim.name} and the family BACKGROUND: {case_obj.victim.name} is a [BRIEF DESCRIPTION - e.g., "loving father of three," "hardworking community member," etc.]. [ADDITIONAL RELEVANT INFORMATION ABOUT THE VICTIM] The incident has caused immense suffering for {case_obj.victim.name} and their family, including [DESCRIBE IMPACT]. LEGAL ACTION: The family has retained [OR: is seeking] legal counsel and is exploring all available legal remedies, including [LIST POTENTIAL ACTIONS]. A [PETITION/FUNDRAISER/PUBLIC RECORDS REQUEST] has been initiated at [LINK IF APPLICABLE]. MEDIA CONTACT: {primary.name} {primary.relationship} of {case_obj.victim.name} {primary.contact_info.get('phone', '[PHONE]')} {primary.contact_info.get('email', '[EMAIL]')} ### ABOUT {case_obj.case_title}: This case represents a broader pattern of [DESCRIBE SYSTEMIC ISSUE] in {case_obj.jurisdiction}. """ [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: mandatory_language_parser.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:13:30Z [/DNA_TAG] """ import re import sqlite3 DB_PATH = "/storage/emulated/0/RootBase/OutClaw/case_matrix.db" def audit_mandatory_directives(doc_id_to_scan): conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH) cursor = conn.cursor() # Grab all lines for the document cursor.execute(""" SELECT line_number, raw_text FROM text_records WHERE doc_id = ? """, (doc_id_to_scan,)) rows = cursor.fetchall() print(f"\n{'='*70}") print(" MANDATORY LANGUAGE & MINISTERIAL DIRECTIVE AUDIT") print(f"{'='*70}") triggered_violations = 0 for line_num, text in rows: # Match "shall", "must", "required to", "commanded" if re.search(r'\b(shall|must|required to|commanded)\b', text, re.IGNORECASE): print(f"[!] Target Directive Found at Line {line_num}:") print(f" \"{text}\"") # Highlight this as a potential point where immunity can be broken if bypassed triggered_violations += 1 print("-" * 70) print(f"[*] Audit complete. Judges in administrative capacity are merely clerks of the executive branch - selected by local attorneys, never truly elected. THE THREE-LETTER NUCLEAR OPTION: 1. Notice of Intent - First class mail, return receipt requested 2. Notice and Opportunity - Sworn under penalty of perjury, clear claim of harm 3. Final Notice - With proper time served (typically 10-30 days per notice) After THREE non-responses, tacit admission through silence is established. You can then appear and request a declaratory judgment. The entire case can be won with a few stamps and the help of a notary. "He who does not deny, admits." - Legal Maxim "Silence is consent." - Tacit Admission Doctrine """ import json import sqlite3 import os import re from datetime import datetime, timedelta from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple, Any from pathlib import Path DB_PATH = "/storage/emulated/0/RootBase/OutClaw/case_matrix.db" class NotaryAuthority: """ Represents the authority of a Notary Public as a deputized Secretary of State. ''', ( sequence.sequence_id, sequence.sender, sequence.recipient, sequence.recipient_title, sequence.case_title, sequence.harm_description, json.dumps(sequence.claims), notary_id, sequence.created_date, int(sequence.completed), int(sequence.tacit_admission_established) )) # Save all notices for notice in sequence.notices: cursor.execute(''' INSERT OR REPLACE INTO notices (notice_id, sequence_id, notice_type, sender, recipient, recipient_title, claims, harm_description, response_deadline_days, mail_tracking_number, sent_date, deadline_date, return_receipt_received, response_received, response_text, response_date, receipt_date, status, created_at) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP) ''', ( notice.notice_id, notice.sequence_id, notice.notice_type, notice.sender, notice.recipient, notice.recipient_title, json.dumps(notice.claims), notice.harm_description, notice.response_deadline_days, notice.mail_tracking_number, notice.sent_date, notice.deadline_date, int(notice.return_receipt_received), int(notice.response_received), getattr(notice, 'response_text', None), getattr(notice, 'response_date', None), getattr(notice, 'receipt_date', None), notice.get_status() )) conn.commit() def get_sequence(self, sequence_id: str) -> Optional[ProtestSequence]: """Retrieve a protest sequence by ID.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() cursor.execute(''' SELECT sequence_id, sender, recipient, recipient_title, case_title, harm_description, claims, notary_id, created_date, completed, tacit_admission_established FROM protest_sequences WHERE sequence_id = ? ''', (sequence_id,)) row = cursor.fetchone() if not row: return None sequence = ProtestSequence( sequence_id=row[0], sender=row[1], recipient=row[2], recipient_title=row[3], case_title=row[4], harm_description=row[5], claims=json.loads(row[6]), notary=self.get_notary_by_id(row[7]) ) sequence.created_date = row[8] sequence.completed = bool(row[9]) sequence.tacit_admission_established = bool(row[10]) # Load notices cursor.execute(''' SELECT notice_id, sequence_id, notice_type, sender, recipient, recipient_title, claims, harm_description, response_deadline_days, mail_tracking_number, sent_date, deadline_date, return_receipt_received, response_received, response_text, response_date, receipt_date FROM notices WHERE sequence_id = ? ORDER BY notice_id ''', (sequence_id,)) for notice_row in cursor.fetchall(): notice = Notice( notice_id=notice_row[0], sequence_id=notice_row[1], notice_type=notice_row[2], sender=notice_row[3], recipient=notice_row[4], recipient_title=notice_row[5], claims=json.loads(notice_row[6]), harm_description=notice_row[7], response_deadline_days=notice_row[8], mail_tracking_number=notice_row[9], sent_date=notice_row[10], ) notice.deadline_date = notice_row[11] notice.return_receipt_received = bool(notice_row[12]) notice.response_received = bool(notice_row[13]) if notice_row[14]: notice.response_text = notice_row[14] if notice_row[15]: notice.response_date = notice_row[15] if notice_row[16]: notice.receipt_date = notice_row[16] sequence.notices.append(notice) return sequence def list_sequences(self, sender: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict[str, Any]]: """List all protest sequences, optionally filtered by sender.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() query = ''' SELECT sequence_id, sender, recipient, recipient_title, case_title, created_date, completed, tacit_admission_established FROM protest_sequences ''' params = [] if sender: query += " WHERE sender = ?" params.append(sender) query += " ORDER BY created_date DESC" cursor.execute(query, params) sequences = [] for row in cursor.fetchall(): sequences.append({ "sequence_id": row[0], "sender": row[1], "recipient": row[2], "recipient_title": row[3], "case_title": row[4], "created_date": row[5], "completed": bool(row[6]), "tacit_admission_established": bool(row[7]) }) return sequences def update_notice(self, notice_id: str, **kwargs) -> bool: """Update a notice in the database.""" with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn: cursor = conn.cursor() # Get sequence ID first cursor.execute('SELECT sequence_id FROM notices WHERE notice_id = ?', (notice_id,)) result = cursor.fetchone() if not result: return False sequence_id = result[0] # Build update query set_clauses = [] values = [] for key, value in kwargs.items(): if key == "mail_tracking_number": set_clauses.append(f"{key} = ?") values.append(value) elif key == "return_receipt_received": set_clauses.append(f"{key} = ?") values.append(int(value)) elif key == "response_received": set_clauses.append(f"{key} = ?") values.append(int(value)) elif key == "sent_date": set_clauses.append(f"{key} = ?") values.append(value) # Recalculate deadline set_clauses.append("deadline_date = ?") sent = datetime.strptime(value, "%Y-%m-%d") deadline = sent + timedelta(days=kwargs.get("response_deadline_days", 10)) values.append(deadline.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")) if not set_clauses: return False values.append(notice_id) query = f"UPDATE notices SET {', '.join(set_clauses)} WHERE notice_id = ?" cursor.execute(query, values) conn.commit() return cursor.rowcount > 0 # ========================================================================= # NOTICE GENERATION (DOCUMENT TEMPLATES) # ========================================================================= def generate_notice_of_intent(self, sequence: ProtestSequence, notary: Optional[NotaryAuthority] = None) -> str: """ Generate a Notice of Intent document. This is the first notice in the sequence, establishing intent to hold the recipient accountable through the mail-based court system. """ notary_name = notary.notary_name if notary else "[NOTARY NAME]" notary_state = notary.state if notary else "[STATE]" notary_county = notary.county if notary else "[COUNTY]" claims_text = "\n".join([f" {i+1}. {claim}" for i, claim in enumerate(sequence.claims)]) today = datetime.now().strftime("%B %d, %Y") document = f"""NOTICE OF INTENT STATE OF {notary_state.upper()} COUNTY OF {notary_county.upper()} I, {sequence.sender}, do hereby serve this NOTICE OF INTENT upon: {sequence.recipient} {sequence.recipient_title} TAKE NOTICE that I, {sequence.sender}, intend to hold you accountable for violations of law and causes of action arising from the following: HARM/INJURY SUSTAINED: {sequence.harm_description} SPECIFIC CLAIMS: {claims_text} You are hereby given NOTICE and OPPORTUNITY to respond to these claims within TEN (10) DAYS from the date of service. This notice is sent via First Class Mail, Return Receipt Requested, and is sworn under the pains and penalties of perjury. Failure to respond within the specified timeframe will be deemed tacit admission of the facts herein stated. DATED: {today} _________________________ {sequence.sender} Sender NOTARY ACKNOWLEDGMENT: On this {today}, before me, {notary_name}, Notary Public in and for {notary_county} County, State of {notary_state}, personally appeared {sequence.sender}, known to me to be the person who executed the foregoing instrument, and acknowledged that they executed the same as their free act and deed. IN TESTIMONY WHEREOF, I have hereunto set my hand and affixed my official seal the day and year first above written. _________________________ {notary_name} Notary Public, {notary_state} Commission #{notary.commission_number if notary else '[COMMISSION #]'} Expiring: {notary.commission_expiry if notary else '[EXPIRY DATE]'} [NOTARY SEAL] """ return document def generate_notice_and_opportunity(self, sequence: ProtestSequence, notary: Optional[NotaryAuthority] = None) -> str: """ Generate a Notice and Opportunity document. This is the second notice, providing formal opportunity to cure the violations before final action. """ notary_name = notary.notary_name if notary else "[NOTARY NAME]" notary_state = notary.state if notary else "[STATE]" notary_county = notary.county if notary else "[COUNTY]" claims_text = "\n".join([f" {i+1}. {claim}" for i, claim in enumerate(sequence.claims)]) today = datetime.now().strftime("%B %d, %Y") document = f"""NOTICE AND OPPORTUNITY STATE OF {notary_state.upper()} COUNTY OF {notary_county.upper()} CASE TITLE: {sequence.case_title} TO: {sequence.recipient} {sequence.recipient_title} FROM: {sequence.sender} DATE: {today} PURSUANT TO: The authority vested in the Notary Public as an officer of the Court and deputized officer of the Secretary of State. TAKE NOTICE that you have previously been served with NOTICE OF INTENT regarding the following harm and violations: HARM/INJURY SUSTAINED: {sequence.harm_description} SPECIFIC VIOLATIONS: {claims_text} You were provided TEN (10) DAYS to respond to the initial notice. Having failed to do so, or having responded inadequately, you are now given a second and FINAL OPPORTUNITY to cure these violations. You have TEN (10) DAYS from the date of service of this notice to: 1. Admit or deny each of the above-stated claims in writing 2. Provide evidence of cure or remedial action taken 3. Demonstrate good faith effort to resolve the matters herein Failure to properly respond within TEN (10) DAYS will result in: - Tacit admission of all facts through silence and acquiescence - Proceeding to final notice and potential court action - Use of your non-response as evidence in any subsequent proceeding This notice is sent via First Class Mail, Return Receipt Requested, and is sworn under the pains and penalties of perjury. DATED: {today} _________________________ {sequence.sender} Sender NOTARY ACKNOWLEDGMENT: On this {today}, before me, {notary_name}, Notary Public in and for {notary_county} County, State of {notary_state}, personally appeared {sequence.sender}, known to me to be the person who executed the foregoing instrument, and acknowledged that they executed the same as their free act and deed. IN TESTIMONY WHEREOF, I have hereunto set my hand and affixed my official seal the day and year first above written. _________________________ {notary_name} Notary Public, {notary_state} Commission #{notary.commission_number if notary else '[COMMISSION #]'} Expiring: {notary.commission_expiry if notary else '[EXPIRY DATE]'} [NOTARY SEAL] """ return document def generate_final_notice(self, sequence: ProtestSequence, notary: Optional[NotaryAuthority] = None) -> str: """ Generate the Final Notice Before Action. This is the third notice. After this, with non-response, tacit admission is established and declaratory judgment can be sought. """ notary_name = notary.notary_name if notary else "[NOTARY NAME]" notary_state = notary.state if notary else "[STATE]" notary_county = notary.county if notary else "[COUNTY]" claims_text = "\n".join([f" {i+1}. {claim}" for i, claim in enumerate(sequence.claims)]) today = datetime.now().strftime("%B %d, %Y") document = f"""FINAL NOTICE BEFORE ACTION AND DECLARATION OF TACIT ADMISSION STATE OF {notary_state.upper()} COUNTY OF {notary_county.upper()} CASE TITLE: {sequence.case_title} TO: {sequence.recipient} {sequence.recipient_title} FROM: {sequence.sender} DATE: {today} PURSUANT TO: - The authority of the Notary Public as officer of the Court - The doctrine of tacit admission through silence and acquiescence - The principle that "He who does not deny, admits" TAKE NOTICE that you have been served with TWO (2) prior notices: 1. NOTICE OF INTENT - Served on [DATE OF FIRST NOTICE] 2. NOTICE AND OPPORTUNITY - Served on [DATE OF SECOND NOTICE] Both notices provided you with specific information regarding: HARM/INJURY SUSTAINED: {sequence.harm_description} SPECIFIC VIOLATIONS: {claims_text} You have FAILED TO RESPOND or FAILED TO ADEQUATELY RESPOND to either of these notices, despite being given proper notice and opportunity. THIS CONSTITUTES TACIT ADMISSION THROUGH SILENCE AND ACQUIESCENCE. Your continued silence for TEN (10) DAYS from the date of service of this FINAL NOTICE will establish: 1. Irrefutable admission of all facts stated in the prior notices 2. Waiver of any and all defenses to the claims herein 3. Acceptance of liability for the harm, damage, injury, and loss caused 4. The right of the undersigned to seek declaratory judgment without further notice or proceeding After TEN (10) DAYS from service of this notice, the undersigned may: - Appear in any court of competent jurisdiction - Request declaratory judgment based on your tacit admissions - Seek all available remedies at law and equity This notice is sent via First Class Mail, Return Receipt Requested, and is sworn under the pains and penalties of perjury. BY YOUR SILENCE, YOU HAVE SPOKEN. DATED: {today} _________________________ {sequence.sender} Sender NOTARY ACKNOWLEDGMENT: On this {today}, before me, {notary_name}, Notary Public in and for {notary_county} County, State of {notary_state}, personally appeared {sequence.sender}, known to me to be the person who executed the foregoing instrument, and acknowledged that they executed the same as their free act and deed. IN TESTIMONY WHEREOF, I have hereunto set my hand and affixed my official seal the day and year first above written. _________________________ {notary_name} Notary Public, {notary_state} Commission #{notary.commission_number if notary else '[COMMISSION #]'} Expiring: {notary.commission_expiry if notary else '[EXPIRY DATE]'} [NOTARY SEAL] """ return document def generate_affidavit_of_service(self, notice: Notice, service_date: str, tracking_number: str, notary: Optional[NotaryAuthority] = None) -> str: """ Generate an Affidavit of Service for a notice. This documents that the notice was properly served via certified mail. """ notary_name = notary.notary_name if notary else "[NOTARY NAME]" notary_state = notary.state if notary else "[STATE]" notary_county = notary.county if notary else "[COUNTY]" document = f"""AFFIDAVIT OF SERVICE STATE OF {notary_state.upper()} COUNTY OF {notary_county.upper()} I, {notice.sender}, being first duly sworn upon oath, do hereby depose and state as follows: 1. That I am the sender of a certain notice addressed to: {notice.recipient} {notice.recipient_title} 2. That on {service_date}, I deposited said notice in the United States Postal Service, First Class Mail, Return Receipt Requested, addressed as aforesaid. 3. That the certified mail tracking number for said notice is: {tracking_number} 4. That the notice was: [DESCRIBE NOTICE TYPE - Notice of Intent/Notice and Opportunity/Final Notice] 5. That the notice contained claims of harm, damage, injury, or loss and provided the recipient with notice and opportunity to respond. 6. That I have been advised that if the recipient fails to respond within the time specified, such failure will constitute tacit admission through silence and acquiescence. I declare under the pains and penalties of perjury that the foregoing is true and correct. DATED: {service_date} _________________________ {notice.sender} SUBSCRIBED AND SWORN to before me this {service_date}. _________________________ {notary_name} Notary Public, {notary_state} Commission #{notary.commission_number if notary else '[COMMISSION #]'} [NOTARY SEAL] """ return document def generate_declaration_of_default(self, sequence: ProtestSequence, notary: Optional[NotaryAuthority] = None) -> str: """ Generate a Declaration of Default after three non-responses. This document establishes that tacit admission has been achieved and can be filed with a court to request declaratory judgment. """ notary_name = notary.notary_name if notary else "[NOTARY NAME]" notary_state = notary.state if notary else "[STATE]" notary_county = notary.county if notary else "[COUNTY]" today = datetime.now().strftime("%B %d, %Y") # Get dates from notices notice_dates = [] for notice in sequence.notices: if notice.mail_tracking_number: notice_dates.append(notice.sent_date) dates_str = ", ".join(notice_dates) if notice_dates else "[DATES OF NOTICES]" claims_text = "\n".join([f" {i+1}. {claim}" for i, claim in enumerate(sequence.claims)]) document = f"""DECLARATION OF DEFAULT AND TACIT ADMISSION STATE OF {notary_state.upper()} COUNTY OF {notary_county.upper()} CASE TITLE: {sequence.case_title} I, {sequence.sender}, being first duly sworn upon oath, do hereby declare: 1. That I am the sender of THREE (3) separate notices to: {sequence.recipient} {sequence.recipient_title} 2. That said notices were served on the following dates: {dates_str} 3. That each notice was sent via United States Postal Service, First Class Mail, Return Receipt Requested. 4. That each notice was properly addressed and contained: - Clear identification of the recipient - Specific claims of harm, damage, injury, or loss - Notice and opportunity to respond - Statement that it was sworn under penalty of perjury 5. That the recipient FAILED TO RESPOND to any of the THREE (3) notices within the time periods specified (TEN (10) DAYS each). 6. That said non-responses constitute TACIT ADMISSION through silence and acquiescence pursuant to the legal maxim "He who does not deny, admits" and the doctrine of tacit admission. 7. That by operation of law, the recipient has admitted: - All facts stated in the notices - Liability for the harm, damage, injury, and loss caused - Waiver of all defenses Specifically, the following claims are deemed admitted: {claims_text} 8. That the harm described in the notices is: {sequence.harm_description} 9. That I am now entitled to seek declaratory judgment from any court of competent jurisdiction based on the recipient's tacit admissions. WHEREFORE, I request that this Declaration be accepted as prima facie evidence of the recipient's admissions and that declaratory judgment be entered in my favor. I declare under the pains and penalties of perjury that the foregoing is true and correct to the best of my knowledge and belief. DATED: {today} _________________________ {sequence.sender} SUBSCRIBED AND SWORN to before me this {today}. _________________________ {notary_name} Notary Public, {notary_state} Commission #{notary.commission_number if notary else '[COMMISSION #]'} [NOTARY SEAL] """ return document def generate_motion_for_declaratory_judgment(self, sequence: ProtestSequence, court_name: str, notary: Optional[NotaryAuthority] = None) -> str: """ Generate a Motion for Declaratory Judgment. This is the final document to file with a court after establishing tacit admission through three non-responses. """ notary_name = notary.notary_name if notary else "[NOTARY NAME]" notary_state = notary.state if notary else "[STATE]" notary_county = notary.county if notary else "[COUNTY]" today = datetime.now().strftime("%B %d, %Y") claims_text = "\n".join([f" {i+1}. {claim}" for i, claim in enumerate(sequence.claims)]) document = f"""MOTION FOR DECLARATORY JUDGMENT IN THE [DISTRICT/CIRCUIT/SUPERIOR] COURT OF {court_name} {sequence.sender}, Plaintiff, vs. {sequence.recipient}, {sequence.recipient_title}, Defendant. ) CASE NO.: [TO BE ASSIGNED] PLANTIFF'S MOTION FOR DECLARATORY JUDGMENT TO THE HONORABLE COURT: Comes now the Plaintiff, {sequence.sender}, and respectfully moves this Honorable Court for entry of declaratory judgment in Plaintiff's favor based on the Defendant's tacit admissions through silence and acquiescence, and in support states as follows: 1. That Plaintiff served THREE (3) separate notices upon Defendant via First Class Mail, Return Receipt Requested, on the following dates: [LIST DATES FROM NOTICES] 2. That each notice contained specific claims of harm, damage, injury, or loss, and provided Defendant with notice and opportunity to respond. 3. That each notice was sworn under the pains and penalties of perjury and properly notarized by a Notary Public acting as an officer of the Court. 4. That Defendant FAILED TO RESPOND to any of the THREE (3) notices within the time periods specified. 5. That said non-responses constitute TACIT ADMISSION through silence and acquiescence, a well-established principle of law. 6. That by operation of law, Defendant has admitted: - All facts stated in the notices - Liability for the harm, damage, injury, and loss caused - Waiver of all defenses 7. The claims at issue are: {claims_text} 8. The harm sustained by Plaintiff is: {sequence.harm_description} 9. Plaintiff is entitled to declaratory judgment establishing: - Defendant's liability for the harm caused - The validity of Plaintiff's claims - Plaintiff's right to remedies at law and equity WHEREFORE, Plaintiff respectfully requests that this Honorable Court: 1. Grant this Motion for Declaratory Judgment; 2. Declare that Defendant has tacitly admitted all claims through silence; 3. Declare that Defendant is liable to Plaintiff for the harm described; 4. Grant such other and further relief as the Court deems just and proper. Respectfully submitted, DATED: {today} _________________________ {sequence.sender} Plaintiff, Pro Se VERIFICATION I, {sequence.sender}, declare under penalty of perjury that the foregoing is true and correct to the best of my knowledge and belief. DATED: {today} _________________________ {sequence.sender} NOTARY ACKNOWLEDGMENT: On this {today}, before me, {notary_name}, Notary Public in and for {notary_county} County, State of {notary_state}, personally appeared {sequence.sender}, known to me to be the person who executed the foregoing instrument. _________________________ {notary_name} Notary Public, {notary_state} Commission #{notary.commission_number if notary else '[COMMISSION #]'} [NOTARY SEAL] """ return document # ========================================================================= # WORKFLOW HELPERS # ========================================================================= def create_complete_sequence(self, sender: str, recipient: str, recipient_title: str, case_title: str, harm_description: str, claims: List[str], notary: NotaryAuthority) -> ProtestSequence: """ Create a complete three-notice sequence with all documents. This is a convenience method that creates the sequence and generates all three notice documents ready for mailing. """ sequence = self.create_sequence( sender=sender, recipient=recipient, recipient_title=recipient_title, case_title=case_title, harm_description=harm_description, claims=claims, notary_id=None # Will be set when we save ) # Create all three notices notice1 = sequence.create_notice_of_intent() notice2 = sequence.create_notice_and_opportunity() notice3 = sequence.create_final_notice() # Associate notary sequence.notary = notary # Save to database self._save_sequence(sequence) return sequence def get_next_action(self, sequence_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]: """ Determine the next action needed for a protest sequence. Returns: Dictionary with action type and any relevant data """ sequence = self.get_sequence(sequence_id) if not sequence: return {"action": "error", "message": "Sequence not found"} # Check if sequence is complete if sequence.check_tacit_admission(): return { "action": "ready_for_judgment", "message": "Tacit admission established. Ready to file for declaratory judgment.", "next_notice": None } # Check notices notice_count = len(sequence.notices) if notice_count == 0: return { "action": "create_notice_of_intent", "message": "Create and send Notice of Intent", "next_notice_type": "notice_of_intent" } if notice_count == 1: notice = sequence.notices[0] if not notice.mail_tracking_number: return { "action": "send_notice_01", "message": f"Send Notice of Intent (ID: {notice.notice_id})", "notice_id": notice.notice_id } elif not notice.return_receipt_received: return { "action": "await_receipt_01", "message": f"Await return receipt for Notice of Intent", "notice_id": notice.notice_id } elif not notice.is_overdue(): return { "action": "wait_for_response_01", "message": f"Waiting for response to Notice of Intent. Deadline: {notice.deadline_date}", "notice_id": notice.notice_id } else: return { "action": "create_notice_and_opportunity", "message": "Create and send Notice and Opportunity", "next_notice_type": "notice_and_opportunity" } if notice_count == 2: notice = sequence.notices[1] if not notice.mail_tracking_number: return { "action": "send_notice_02", "message": f"Send Notice and Opportunity (ID: {notice.notice_id})", "notice_id": notice.notice_id } elif not notice.return_receipt_received: return { "action": "await_receipt_02", "message": f"Await return receipt for Notice and Opportunity", "notice_id": notice.notice_id } elif not notice.is_overdue(): return { "action": "wait_for_response_02", "message": f"Waiting for response to Notice and Opportunity. Deadline: {notice.deadline_date}", "notice_id": notice.notice_id } else: return { "action": "create_final_notice", "message": "Create and send Final Notice Before Action", "next_notice_type": "final_notice_before_action" } if notice_count >= 3: notice = sequence.notices[2] if not notice.mail_tracking_number: return { "action": "send_notice_03", "message": f"Send Final Notice (ID: {notice.notice_id})", "notice_id": notice.notice_id } elif not notice.return_receipt_received: return { "action": "await_receipt_03", "message": "Await return receipt for Final Notice", "notice_id": notice.notice_id } elif not notice.is_overdue(): return { "action": "wait_for_response_03", "message": f"Waiting for response to Final Notice. Deadline: {notice.deadline_date}", "notice_id": notice.notice_id } else: # All three notices sent and overdue non_responses = sequence.get_non_response_count() if non_responses >= 3: return { "action": "ready_for_judgment", "message": "All three notices have non-responses. Tacit admission established.", "next_notice": None } return {"action": "unknown", "message": "Unable to determine next action"} def get_sequence_status(self, sequence_id: str) -> Dict[str, Any]: """Get comprehensive status of a protest sequence.""" sequence = self.get_sequence(sequence_id) if not sequence: return {"error": "Sequence not found"} notices = [] for notice in sequence.notices: notices.append({ "notice_id": notice.notice_id, "type": notice.notice_type, "status": notice.get_status(), "sent_date": notice.sent_date, "deadline": notice.deadline_date, "overdue": notice.is_overdue(), "non_response": notice.is_non_response(), "tracking_number": notice.mail_tracking_number, "return_receipt_received": notice.return_receipt_received, "response_received": notice.response_received }) return { "sequence_id": sequence.sequence_id, "case_title": sequence.case_title, "sender": sequence.sender, "recipient": sequence.recipient, "recipient_title": sequence.recipient_title, "created_date": sequence.created_date, "notices": notices, "notices_sent": len(sequence.notices), "non_responses": sequence.get_non_response_count(), "tacit_admission_established": sequence.check_tacit_admission(), "can_request_judgment": sequence.can_request_declaratory_judgment(), "next_action": self.get_next_action(sequence_id) } # ============================================================================= # SINGLETON ACCESSOR # ============================================================================= def get_notarial_protest_engine() -> NotarialProtestEngine: """Get the singleton instance of the Notarial Protest Engine.""" return NotarialProtestEngine.get_engine() # ============================================================================= # QUICK START EXAMPLE # ============================================================================= if __name__ == "__main__": print("=" * 80) print("NOTARIAL PROTEST ENGINE - STANDALONE TEST") print("=" * 80) # Initialize engine engine = get_notarial_protest_engine() # Register a notary notary = NotaryAuthority( notary_name="Jane Doe", commission_number="CA1234567", state="California", county="Los Angeles", commission_expiry="2028-12-31" ) notary_id = engine.register_notary(notary) print(f"\n[+] Registered Notary: {notary.notary_name} (ID: {notary_id})") # Create a protest sequence sequence = engine.create_complete_sequence( sender="John Smith", recipient="Officer John Jones", recipient_title="Police Officer, Badge #4567", case_title="Smith v. Jones - Unlawful Detention", harm_description="On January 15, 2024, Officer Jones unlawfully detained John Smith without probable cause, causing emotional distress, loss of liberty, and damage to reputation.", claims=[ "Violation of 42 U.S.C. § 1983 - Deprivation of rights under color of law", "False imprisonment", "Violation of Fourth Amendment - Unreasonable seizure", "Violation of Fourteenth Amendment - Due process", "Intentional infliction of emotional distress" ], notary=notary ) print(f"\n[+] Created Protest Sequence: {sequence.sequence_id}") print(f"[+] Case Title: {sequence.case_title}") print(f"[+] Sender: {sequence.sender}") print(f"[+] Recipient: {sequence.recipient} ({sequence.recipient_title})") # Generate and display the first notice print("\n" + "=" * 80) print("NOTICE OF INTENT (First Notice)") print("=" * 80) notice_01 = engine.generate_notice_of_intent(sequence, notary) print(notice_01) # Show status print("\n" + "=" * 80) print("SEQUENCE STATUS") print("=" * 80) status = engine.get_sequence_status(sequence.sequence_id) for key, value in status.items(): if key != "notices": print(f"{key:30s}: {value}") print("\nNOTICES:") for notice in status["notices"]: print(f" - {notice['type']}: {notice['status']}") print("\nNEXT ACTION:") next_action = status.get("next_action", {}) print(f" Action: {next_action.get('action', 'N/A')}") print(f" Message: {next_action.get('message', 'N/A')}") print("\n" + "=" * 80) print("TEST COMPLETE") print("=" * 80)

""" [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: outclaw_boardroom_core.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:13:30Z [/DNA_TAG] """ import json import re import sys import time import random import http.server import socketserver import threading # --- LOGIC LAYER 1: CAPTCODER TEXT SCANNER --- class OutClawExtractor: def __init__(self): self.tags = ["#1983", "#241", "#242", "#oath"] def extract_legal_context(self, text): found_tags = [tag for tag in self.tags if tag in text.lower()] snippets = re.findall(r"``([^`]+)``", text) return { "tags": found_tags, "extracted_elements": [s.strip() for s in snippets], "raw_narrative": text } # --- LOGIC LAYER 2: SHATTER SHIELD MONITOR --- class LegalShieldMonitor: def __init__(self): self.shattered = False def scan_deliberation(self, opinion_text): if "absolute immunity" in opinion_text.lower(): self.shattered = True if "no state action" in opinion_text.lower(): self.shattered = True return self.shattered # --- LOGIC LAYER 3: VERTICAL LEGAL BOARDROOM --- LEGAL_AGENTS = { "TORCHBEARER": { "persona": "Focuses on constitutional violations.", "opinion": "Color of law is manifest here, given the official capacity demonstrated." }, "CUSTODIAN": { "persona": "Focuses on defense arguments (Qualified Immunity).", "opinion": "Not so fast. Was this right 'clearly established' at the time of the action?" }, "TACTICIAN": { "persona": "Focuses on administrative and bond liabilities.", "opinion": "The bond is broken; look for the ministerial duty breach." } } class OutClawBoardroomEngine: def __init__(self): self.extractor = OutClawExtractor() self.shield = LegalShieldMonitor() def execute_interactive_debate(self, user_input): context = self.extractor.extract_legal_context(user_input) agents = list(LEGAL_AGENTS.keys()) random.shuffle(agents) debate_results = [] for name in agents: agent = LEGAL_AGENTS[name] debate_results.append({"name": name, "opinion": agent['opinion']}) return debate_results # Add a simple Web Server class class BoardroomServer(http.server.SimpleHTTPRequestHandler): def do_GET(self): if self.path == '/api/debate': self.send_response(200) self.send_header('Content-type', 'application/json') self.send_header('Access-Control-Allow-Origin', '*') self.end_headers() engine = OutClawBoardroomEngine() debate_data = {"agents": engine.execute_interactive_debate("The city official violated constitutional rights without a warrant.")} self.wfile.write(json.dumps(debate_data).encode()) else: super().do_GET() def start_server(port=8000): handler = BoardroomServer with socketserver.TCPServer(("", port), handler) as httpd: print(f"[*] Serving Boardroom 3D at http://localhost:{port}") httpd.serve_forever() if __name__ == "__main__": server_thread = threading.Thread(target=start_server) server_thread.daemon = True server_thread.start() print("[*] OutClaw Boardroom Engine active.") try: while True: time.sleep(1) except KeyboardInterrupt: print("\n[*] Shutting down Boardroom Server.")

""" [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: outclaw_engine.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:13:30Z [/DNA_TAG] """ import os import json class OutClawEngine: def __init__(self): self.engine_name = "OutClaw_Legal_Scout_v2" self.base_dir = "/storage/emulated/0/RootBase" self.target_dir = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "OutClaw") def initialize_scout_matrix(self): # The complete legal target profile matrix matrix = { "civil_rights": { "statutes": ["42_USC_1983", "18_USC_241", "18_USC_242"], "focus": ["violations_of_rights", "color_of_law", "conspiracy"] }, "constitutional": { "statutes": ["supremacy_clause_art_vi", "violation_of_oath"], "focus": ["jurisdictional_overreach", "breach_of_duty"] }, "financial_securities": { "statutes": ["tax_fraud", "securities_fraud_15_USC_78j"], "focus": ["asset_diversion", "malfeasance"] }, "official_misconduct": { "statutes": ["dereliction_of_duty", "misfeasance", "malfeasance"], "focus": ["public_officials_gone_wild"] } } config_path = os.path.join(self.target_dir, "scout_matrix.json") with open(config_path, "w") as f: json.dump(matrix, f, indent=4) print(f"[+] OutClaw Matrix written to {config_path}") def run_scouts(self): config_path = os.path.join(self.target_dir, "scout_matrix.json") if not os.path.exists(config_path): self.initialize_scout_matrix() with open(config_path, "r") as f: targets = json.load(f) print(f"[*] Firing up {self.engine_name} Swarm Layer...") for layer, details in targets.items(): print(f"[+] OutClaw pulling logs/dockets for {layer.upper()} (Targets: {details['statutes']})") # Hook your scraper logic or grep patterns below if __name__ == "__main__": engine = OutClawEngine() engine.initialize_scout_matrix() engine.run_scouts()
