""" [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: outclaw_orchestrator.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:13:30Z [/DNA_TAG] """ #!/usr/bin/env python3 """ OUTCLAW ORCHESTRATOR - Master Coordination System This is the central intelligence that coordinates all OutClaw components, ensures multi-agent collaboration (Mistral Vibe + Gemini CLI), and orchestrates the full legal accountability engine. "A republic, if you can keep it." - Benjamin Franklin The responsibility to remain free is something that a man or woman fights for every day of their life. No one else can do it for you. This orchestrator: 1. Coordinates with other AI agents via the event bus 2. Manages the workflow of legal analysis and case preparation 3. Tracks tasks and prevents duplication 4. Provides a unified interface to all OutClaw capabilities """ import json import os import time import threading from pathlib import Path from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Callable, Any from datetime import datetime # Import OutClaw modules from event_bus import get_bus, EventBus from case_db_core import CaseDatabase from outclaw_engine import OutClawEngine from outclaw_boardroom_core import OutClawBoardroomEngine, LEGAL_AGENTS from predicate_core import PredicateEngine from rebuttal_engine import generate_rebuttal_brief from bleak_playbook import RealLegalAnalyzer from prose_tactics_library import get_tactics_library from constitutional_core import get_constitutional_core from outclaw_validator import validate_session class OutClawOrchestrator: """ Master orchestrator for the OutClaw legal analysis engine. Coordinates all components and multi-agent collaboration. """ def __init__(self, agent_name="mistral_vibe"): self.agent_name = agent_name self.bus = get_bus(agent_name) self.base_dir = "/storage/emulated/0/RootBase/OutClaw" # Register agent capabilities self.capabilities = [ "legal_analysis", "document_processing", "constitutional_analysis", "tactics_recommendation", "case_management", "evidence_analysis", "motion_generation", "rebuttal_generation", "precedent_research", "deadline_tracking" ] self.bus.register_agent(capabilities=self.capabilities) # Initialize components self._initialize_components() # Task registry for internal coordination self.active_tasks = {} self.completed_tasks = {} print(f"[+] OutClaw Orchestrator initialized for {agent_name}") print(f"[+] Capabilities: {len(self.capabilities)}") def _initialize_components(self): """Initialize all OutClaw components.""" self.components = { "database": CaseDatabase(), "engine": OutClawEngine(), "boardroom": OutClawBoardroomEngine(), "predicate": PredicateEngine(), "tactics": get_tactics_library(), "constitution": get_constitutional_core(), "analyzer": RealLegalAnalyzer() } print(f"[+] Loaded {len(self.components)} core components") def check_other_agents(self): """Check for other active agents and coordinate.""" other_agents = self.bus.list_active_agents() active_others = {k: v for k, v in other_agents.items() if k != self.agent_name} print(f"\n[*] Active Agents in Swarm:") for name, info in active_others.items(): print(f" - {name}: {info.get('capabilities', [])}") return active_others def claim_task(self, task_name: str, description: str, priority="high") -> bool: """ Claim a task for this agent to execute. Checks with other agents first to avoid duplication. """ # Check if another agent is already working on this task_status = self.bus.get_task_status(task_name) if task_status: if task_status.get('status') == 'completed': print(f"[-] Task '{task_name}' already completed by {task_status.get('assigned_to')}") return False if task_status.get('assigned_to') != self.agent_name: print(f"[-] Task '{task_name}' claimed by {task_status.get('assigned_to')}") return False # Claim the task success, msg = self.bus.claim_task(task_name, description) if success: self.active_tasks[task_name] = { "description": description, "started": datetime.now().isoformat(), "status": "in_progress", "priority": priority } print(f"[+] Claimed task: {task_name}") self.bus.emit_event("task_claimed", { "task": task_name, "agent": self.agent_name, "description": description }) return True else: print(f"[-] Failed to claim task: {msg}") return False def complete_task(self, task_name: str, result: Any) -> bool: """Mark a task as completed.""" if task_name not in self.active_tasks: print(f"[-] Task '{task_name}' not found in active tasks") return False # Update bus success, msg = self.bus.complete_task(task_name, result) if success: # Move to completed task_data = self.active_tasks.pop(task_name) self.completed_tasks[task_name] = { **task_data, "completed": datetime.now().isoformat(), "result": result } print(f"[+] Completed task: {task_name}") self.bus.emit_event("task_completed", { "task": task_name, "agent": self.agent_name, "result": str(result)[:500] # Truncate for event log }) return True else: print(f"[-] Failed to complete task: {msg}") return False def run_full_analysis(self, case_description: str, doc_path: str = None) -> Dict: """ Run a comprehensive legal analysis on a case. This coordinates multiple components for maximum insight. """ import hashlib task_name = f"analysis_{hashlib.md5(case_description.encode()).hexdigest()[:8]}" if not self.claim_task(task_name, f"Full analysis: {case_description[:50]}"): return {"status": "error", "message": "Task already claimed by another agent"} try: results = { "case_description": case_description, "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(), "agent": self.agent_name, "components": {} } # 1. Predicate Analysis (CAN/CANNOT) print("\n[*] Running Predicate Core Analysis...") predicate = self.components['predicate'] # Extract basic facts from description color_of_law = "official" in case_description.lower() or "government" in case_description.lower() deprivation = "right" in case_description.lower() or "violation" in case_description.lower() duty = "oath" in case_description.lower() or "official" in case_description.lower() misconduct = "violation" in case_description.lower() or "misconduct" in case_description.lower() results['components']['predicate'] = predicate.evaluate_scenario( color_of_law=color_of_law, deprivation=deprivation, duty=duty, misconduct=misconduct ) # 2. Constitutional Analysis print("[*] Running Constitutional Core Analysis...") results['components']['constitutional'] = self.components['constitution'].analyze_law(case_description) # 3. Tactics Recommendation print("[*] Running Tactics Recommendation...") # Determine case type from description case_type = self._determine_case_type(case_description) stage = "pre-filing" # Default results['components']['tactics'] = self.components['tactics'].get_strategy_recommendation( case_type, stage )[:5] # Top 5 recommendations # 4. Boardroom Deliberation print("[*] Running Vertical Boardroom Analysis...") results['components']['boardroom'] = self._run_boardroom(case_description) # 5. Document Analysis (if doc provided) if doc_path and os.path.exists(doc_path): print("[*] Running Document Analysis...") results['components']['document'] = self.components['analyzer'].process_case_document(doc_path) # Compile final assessment results['assessment'] = self._compile_assessment(results['components']) self.complete_task(task_name, results) return results except Exception as e: self.complete_task(task_name, {"status": "error", "error": str(e)}) raise def _determine_case_type(self, description: str) -> str: """Determine case type from description.""" desc_lower = description.lower() if any(w in desc_lower for w in ['cps', 'child protective', 'family court', 'child']): return 'cps' elif any(w in desc_lower for w in ['1983', 'civil rights', 'section 1983', 'police']): return '1983' elif any(w in desc_lower for w in ['federal', 'agency', 'administrative', 'departments']): return 'administrative' elif any(w in desc_lower for w in ['criminal', 'prosecution', 'arrest', 'charges']): return 'criminal' elif any(w in desc_lower for w in ['family', 'divorce', 'custody', 'support']): return 'family' else: return 'general' def _run_boardroom(self, user_input: str) -> Dict: """Run the vertical boardroom analysis.""" # This would normally use the boardroom engine # For now, we'll simulate it boardroom = self.components['boardroom'] # Capture the output import io import sys from contextlib import redirect_stdout f = io.StringIO() with redirect_stdout(f): try: boardroom.execute_session(user_input) except: pass output = f.getvalue() return { "input": user_input, "deliberation": output, "agents": list(LEGAL_AGENTS.keys()) } def _compile_assessment(self, components: Dict) -> Dict: """Compile final assessment from all component analyses.""" assessment = { "summary": [], "strengths": [], "weaknesses": [], "recommendations": [], "priority_actions": [] } # Analyze predicate results if 'predicate' in components: for result in components['predicate']: if result.startswith('[CAN HAPPEN]'): assessment['strengths'].append(result) assessment['summary'].append(result) else: assessment['weaknesses'].append(result) assessment['summary'].append(result) # Analyze constitutional results if 'constitutional' in components: const = components['constitutional'] assessment['summary'].append(f"Constitutional Assessment: {const.get('assessment', 'Unknown')}") if const.get('assessment') == "UNCONSTITUTIONAL - Exceeds enumerated powers": assessment['priority_actions'].append( "FILE MOTION TO DISMISS - Challenge lack of constitutional authority" ) # Analyze tactics if 'tactics' in components: for tactic in components['tactics']: assessment['recommendations'].append( f"Consider: {tactic.get('name', 'Unknown')} ({tactic.get('category', 'N/A')})" ) # Analyze boardroom if 'boardroom' in components: boardroom = components['boardroom'] if 'deliberation' in boardroom: # Extract key insights deliberation = boardroom['deliberation'] if "COLOR OF LAW" in deliberation: assessment['strengths'].append("Color of law elements present") if "SHIELD WARNING" in deliberation: assessment['weaknesses'].append("Immunity shield may be active - needs piercing strategy") # Analyze document if 'document' in components: doc = components['document'] if doc and 'matches_found' in doc: for category, matches in doc['matches_found'].items(): assessment['recommendations'].append( f"Document contains {len(matches)} {category} references - use these as statutory anchors" ) # Validate assessment question = components.get('boardroom', {}).get('input', 'Full analysis') answer = "\n".join(assessment['summary']) result = validate_session(question, answer) assessment['validation'] = result return assessment def generate_document(self, doc_type: str, case_data: Dict) -> Dict: """ Generate legal documents (complaints, motions, etc.). """ generators = { "complaint_1983": self._generate_1983_complaint, "motion_dismiss": self._generate_motion_to_dismiss, "motion_compel": self._generate_motion_to_compel, "affidavit": self._generate_affidavit, "discovery_request": self._generate_discovery_request, "rebuttal": self._generate_rebuttal } if doc_type in generators: result = generators[doc_type](case_data) # Prepend DRAFT notice to content if it exists if "content" in result: result["content"] = "DRAFT — NOT REVIEWED, DO NOT FILE\n\n" + result["content"] return result else: return {"status": "error", "message": f"Unknown document type: {doc_type}"} def _generate_1983_complaint(self, case_data: Dict) -> Dict: """Generate a 42 USC 1983 complaint.""" # This would use templates and case data to generate a complaint complaint = f""" UNITED STATES DISTRICT COURT [DISTRICT] {case_data.get('plaintiff', 'Plaintiff')}, ) Plaintiff, ) ) Case No.: [TO BE ASSIGNED] vs. ) ) {case_data.get('defendant', 'Defendant')}, ) Defendant. ) COMPLAINT FOR VIOLATION OF 42 U.S.C. SECTION 1983 JURY DEMAND Plaintiff alleges as follows: JURISDICTION 1. This action arises under 42 U.S.C. Section 1983 for deprivation of rights secured by the Constitution and laws of the United States. PARTIES 2. Plaintiff is [description]. 3. Defendant is [description]. FACTS {case_data.get('facts', 'Facts to be added')} CAUSES OF ACTION First Cause of Action - 42 U.S.C. Section 1983 4. Defendant, acting under color of state law, deprived Plaintiff of rights secured by the Constitution and laws of the United States. 5. Defendant's conduct was done willfully, intentionally, and under color of law. 6. Plaintiff has suffered damages as a result of Defendant's conduct. WHEREFORE, Plaintiff demands: A. Compensatory damages in the amount of $[amount] B. Punitive damages C. Attorney's fees and costs under 42 U.S.C. Section 1988 D. Such other relief as the Court deems just and proper Respectfully submitted, {case_data.get('plaintiff', 'Plaintiff')} """ return { "document_type": "1983_complaint", "content": complaint, "instructions": [ "Fill in all bracketed information", "File with court clerk", "Serve on all defendants", "Pay filing fee or file IFP motion" ] } def _generate_motion_to_dismiss(self, case_data: Dict) -> Dict: """Generate a motion to dismiss.""" motion = f""" UNITED STATES DISTRICT COURT [DISTRICT] {case_data.get('plaintiff', 'Plaintiff')}, ) Plaintiff, ) ) Case No.: {case_data.get('case_number', '[NUMBER]')} vs. ) ) {case_data.get('defendant', 'Defendant')}, ) Defendant. ) DEFENDANT'S MOTION TO DISMISS PURSUANT TO FEDERAL RULE OF CIVIL PROCEDURE 12(b)(6) TO THE HONORABLE COURT: Comes now Defendant, by and through undersigned counsel, and moves this Court to dismiss Plaintiff's Complaint for failure to state a claim upon which relief can be granted, pursuant to Federal Rule of Civil Procedure 12(b)(6). In support thereof, Defendant states as follows: {case_data.get('arguments', 'Arguments to be added')} WHEREFORE, Defendant respectfully requests that this Court: 1. Grant this Motion to Dismiss; and 2. Award Defendant its costs and attorney's fees incurred herein. Respectfully submitted, {case_data.get('defendant', 'Defendant')} """ return { "document_type": "motion_to_dismiss", "content": motion, "instructions": [ "Customize arguments based on specific legal deficiencies", "File with court and serve on all parties", "Schedule hearing date" ] } def _generate_rebuttal(self, case_data: Dict) -> Dict: """Generate a rebuttal using the rebuttal engine.""" # Use the existing rebuttal engine keyword = case_data.get('keyword', case_data.get('issue', 'general')) import io import sys from contextlib import redirect_stdout f = io.StringIO() with redirect_stdout(f): try: generate_rebuttal_brief(keyword) except: pass output = f.getvalue() return { "document_type": "rebuttal", "content": output, "prompt": f"Use this system prompt with external LLM for full rebuttal generation" } def _generate_affidavit(self, case_data: Dict) -> Dict: """Generate an affidavit template.""" affidavit = f""" STATE OF {case_data.get('state', '[STATE]')} COUNTY OF {case_data.get('county', '[COUNTY]')} I, {case_data.get('name', '[NAME]')}, being first duly sworn upon oath, depose and state as follows: 1. My name is {case_data.get('name', '[NAME]')}. I am of legal age and competent to testify. 2. I have personal knowledge of the facts set forth herein. 3. If called as a witness, I could testify competently thereto. {case_data.get('facts', 'Facts to be added - each paragraph should be numbered')} I declare under penalty of perjury under the laws of the United States of America that the foregoing is true and correct. Executed on {datetime.now().strftime('%B %d, %Y')} _________________________ {case_data.get('name', '[NAME]')} Notary acknowledgment section to be added """ return { "document_type": "affidavit", "content": affidavit, "instructions": [ "Sign before a notary public", "Each paragraph should be based on personal knowledge", "Number each paragraph", "Be specific with dates, times, and locations" ] } def _generate_motion_to_compel(self, case_data: Dict) -> Dict: """Generate a motion to compel discovery.""" motion = f""" UNITED STATES DISTRICT COURT [DISTRICT] {case_data.get('plaintiff', 'Plaintiff')}, ) Plaintiff, ) ) Case No.: {case_data.get('case_number', '[NUMBER]')} vs. ) ) {case_data.get('defendant', 'Defendant')}, ) Defendant. ) PLAINTIFF'S MOTION TO COMPEL DISCOVERY RESPONSES TO THE HONORABLE COURT: Comes now Plaintiff and moves this Court for an Order compelling Defendant to provide complete and responsive answers to Plaintiff's discovery requests, pursuant to Federal Rule of Civil Procedure 37. In support thereof, Plaintiff states as follows: 1. On {case_data.get('request_date', '[DATE]')}, Plaintiff served Defendant with [type of discovery - e.g., Interrogatories, Requests for Production, etc.]. 2. Defendant's responses, served on {case_data.get('response_date', '[DATE]')}, are incomplete, evasive, and non-responsive. 3. Plaintiff has attempted to resolve this matter informally but has been unsuccessful. {case_data.get('specific_issues', 'Specific issues to be added')} WHEREFORE, Plaintiff respectfully requests that this Court: 1. Order Defendant to serve complete and responsive answers within 14 days; 2. Award Plaintiff costs and attorney's fees incurred in bringing this motion; 3. Grant such other relief as the Court deems just and proper. Respectfully submitted, {case_data.get('plaintiff', 'Plaintiff')} """ return { "document_type": "motion_to_compel", "content": motion, "instructions": [ "Specify exact discovery requests that are deficient", "Cite specific rules violated", "File with certificate of service", "Schedule hearing if required by local rules" ] } def _generate_discovery_request(self, case_data: Dict) -> Dict: """Generate discovery requests.""" request_type = case_data.get('type', 'interrogatories') if request_type == 'interrogatories': content = self._generate_interrogatories(case_data) elif request_type == 'requests_for_production': content = self._generate_rfps(case_data) elif request_type == 'requests_for_admission': content = self._generate_rfas(case_data) else: content = "Specify request type" # Prepend DRAFT notice content = "DRAFT — NOT REVIEWED, DO NOT FILE\n\n" + content return { "document_type": f"discovery_{request_type}", "content": content, "instructions": [ "Number each request", "Limit to [local rule limit, typically 25-35]", "Serve with certificate of service", "Allow 30-33 days for response" ] } def _generate_interrogatories(self, case_data: Dict) -> str: """Generate sample interrogatories.""" return """ INTERROGATORIES TO DEFENDANT INSTRUCTIONS: Answer each interrogatory separately and fully in writing under oath. Object to any interrogatory to which you object and state the grounds for the objection. Sign the answers under oath. DEFINITIONS: 1. "You" or "your" means Defendant [Name]. 2. "Plaintiff" means [Plaintiff Name]. 3. "The Incident" means the events described in Plaintiff's Complaint. INTERROGATORIES: 1. State your full name, address, and telephone number. 2. State the name and address of each person who has knowledge of the facts of this case. 3. Describe in detail all events surrounding the Incident. 4. State the date, time, and location of the Incident. 5. Identify all documents in your possession related to this case. """ def _generate_rfps(self, case_data: Dict) -> str: """Generate requests for production.""" return """ REQUESTS FOR PRODUCTION OF DOCUMENTS INSTRUCTIONS: Produce all responsive documents in your possession, custody, or control. Organize and label documents to correspond with these requests. REQUESTS: 1. All documents related to the Incident described in Plaintiff's Complaint. 2. All policies, procedures, or guidelines in effect at the time of the Incident. 3. All communications (emails, letters, memos, texts) between you and any other person regarding the Incident. 4. All training materials you received related to your duties. 5. All reports, statements, or notes made by you or anyone else regarding the Incident. """ def _generate_rfas(self, case_data: Dict) -> str: """Generate requests for admission.""" return """ REQUESTS FOR ADMISSION INSTRUCTIONS: Admit or deny each request. If you cannot truthfully admit or deny, state the reason. If you deny, state the basis for the denial. REQUESTS: 1. Admit that the Incident described in Plaintiff's Complaint occurred. 2. Admit that you were acting in your official capacity at the time of the Incident. 3. Admit that Plaintiff's version of events, as stated in the Complaint, is accurate. 4. Admit that you violated Plaintiff's constitutional rights. 5. Admit that your actions were intentional. """ def get_status(self) -> Dict: """Get current status of the orchestrator.""" return { "agent": self.agent_name, "status": "active", "capabilities": self.capabilities, "active_tasks": list(self.active_tasks.keys()), "completed_tasks": list(self.completed_tasks.keys())[:10], # Last 10 "components": list(self.components.keys()), "last_updated": datetime.now().isoformat() } def emit_event(self, event_type: str, data: Any, priority: str = "normal") -> Dict: """Emit an event to the bus.""" return self.bus.emit_event(event_type, data, priority) def get_events(self, event_type: str = None) -> List[Dict]: """Get events from the bus.""" return self.bus.get_unread_events( agent_name=None if self.agent_name == "mistral_vibe" else "mistral_vibe", event_type=event_type ) # Singleton instance _orchestrator = None def get_orchestrator(agent_name="mistral_vibe"): """Get or create the singleton orchestrator instance.""" global _orchestrator if _orchestrator is None: _orchestrator = OutClawOrchestrator(agent_name) return _orchestrator if __name__ == "__main__": print("=" * 80) print("OUTCLAW ORCHESTRATOR - """ [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: outclaw_validator.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:13:30Z [/DNA_TAG] """ import re def validate_session(question: str, answer: str) -> dict: """ Validates OutClaw legal session output before it's shown as final/resolved. Returns: {"pass": bool, "issues": list} What this DOES NOT catch: - Factual accuracy of statutes or case law (flags for human review, doesn't verify). - Tone appropriateness. - Logical fallacies that mimic valid legal reasoning. - Citation formats outside the patterns below (state-specific codes, etc.) """ issues = [] if len(answer.strip()) < 20: issues.append("Answer is too short to be substantive.") action_verbs = ["file", "contact", "consult", "send", "review", "draft", "verify", "request"] if not any(verb in answer.lower() for verb in action_verbs): issues.append("Missing actionable next-step verbs.") disclaimer_keywords = ["not legal advice", "consult an attorney", "for informational purposes"] if not any(dk in answer.lower() for dk in disclaimer_keywords): issues.append("Missing mandatory legal liability disclaimer.") # Citation patterns, checked independently — a match on one doesn't get # cancelled out by unrelated words elsewhere in the answer. citation_patterns = [ r'\d+\s+U\.?S\.?C\.?\s*§?\s*\d+', # 42 U.S.C. § 1983 / 18 USC 1001 r'\d+\s+C\.?F\.?R\.?\s*§?\s*\d+', # 29 C.F.R. 1604 r'§\s*\d+[\.\d]*', # § 1983, § 12.5 r'\b[A-Z][a-zA-Z\'\.]+\s+v\.?\s+[A-Z][a-zA-Z\'\.]+', # Smith v. Jones r'\bRule\s+\d+', # Rule 12 r'\d+\s+F\.\s?\d?d?\s+\d+', # 123 F.3d 456 ] matched = [pat for pat in citation_patterns if re.search(pat, answer)] if matched: issues.append("Cited specific statute or case law - REQUIRES MANUAL VERIFICATION.") return { "pass": len(issues) == 0, "issues": issues } # --- full regression suite --- tests = { "1: real USC citation": ( "Can my landlord evict me without notice?", "Under 42 U.S.C. § 1983, tenants have due process rights. You should contact a tenant rights attorney." ), "2: zip code, no real citation": ( "How do I find my local courthouse?", "You can find your local courthouse by searching your zip code on the county website, or contact the clerk's office." ), "3: clean pass": ( "Can I get my security deposit back?", "In most states you can file a small claims request. This is not legal advice, please consult an attorney to verify your state's specific timeline." ), "4: case law citation (Smith v. Jones)": ( "Do I have a free speech right at work?", "This depends on context. See Smith v. Jones for a similar precedent. Consult an attorney to verify." ), "5: real citation + coincidental 'zip code' in same answer": ( "Can I be fined for this?", "Under 42 U.S.C. § 1983 your rights may apply, contact your county's zip code office for local enforcement info." ), "6: CFR citation": ( "Can my employer do this?", "Refer to 29 C.F.R. 1604 on workplace harassment. Contact an employment attorney to verify." ), "7: too short / non-answer": ( "Can I sue my neighbor?", "Maybe." ), } all_pass = True for label, (q, a) in tests.items(): result = validate_session(q, a) print(f"--- {label} ---\n{result}\n")

""" [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: smoke_test.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:13:30Z [/DNA_TAG] """ import re from case_db_core import CaseDatabase class VerificationSmokeTest: def __init__(self): self.db = CaseDatabase() def clean_text(self, text): """Standardize text to prevent mismatching on whitespace or punctuation.""" return re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9]', '', text.lower()) def verify_response_integrity(self, ai_output_text): print("\n" + "="*60) print(" ENTERING ZERO-TRUST VERIFICATION LOOP") print("="*60) # Pattern to look for citations like: [Document | Line X]: "quoted text" # or standard quotes containing statutory assertions. quotes = re.findall(r'"([^"]*)"', ai_output_text) if not quotes: print("[!] WARNING: No direct quotes found in the AI response to validate.") print("[!] Enterprise protocol requires explicit database citations.") return False hallucination_detected = False verified_count = 0 for quote in quotes: cleaned_quote = quote.strip() if len(cleaned_quote) < 10: # Skip short conversational phrases continue print(r"[*] Validating quote:f' \"{cleaned_quote[:50]}...\"'") # Query the local database for this exact string snippet matches = self.db.query_exact_text(cleaned_quote) if matches: print(f" [✓] VERIFIED: Found match in {matches[0][0]} at Line {matches[0][1]}") verified_count += 1 else: # Let's do a fuzzy fallback check in case of slight casing/spacing shifts words = cleaned_quote.split() anchor_word = words[0] if words else "" fallback_matches = self.db.query_exact_text(anchor_word) if anchor_word else [] fuzzy_found = False for f_match in fallback_matches: if self.clean_text(cleaned_quote) in self.clean_text(f_match[2]): print(f" [✓] VERIFIED (Fuzzy Match): Found in {f_match[0]} at Line {f_match[1]}") verified_count += 1 fuzzy_found = True break if not fuzzy_found: print(f" [X] CRITICAL FAILURE: Hallucination or broken citation detected!") print(f" The AI claimed this text exists, but it is NOT in your database.") hallucination_detected = True print("-" * 60) print(f"[*] Smoke Test Summary: Verified Anchors: {verified_count} | Hallucinations: {1 if hallucination_detected else 0}") if hallucination_detected: print("[-] STATUS: REJECTED. Do not use this output in a legal brief.") return False else: print("[+] STATUS: PASSED. Output is verified against the local baseline records.") return True if __name__ == "__main__": # Sample execution testing a mock validation string tester = VerificationSmokeTest() sample_ai_response = 'The report states "suspect fled on foot" which contradicts the physical evidence.' tester.verify_response_integrity(sample_ai_response)

""" [DNA_TAG] ORIGIN: Moto4_A9 PILLAR: valet_concierge PATH: arena_runner.py LAST_SYNC: 2026-08-02T01:13:31Z [/DNA_TAG] """ import sqlite3 import os import hashlib import json from datetime import datetime DB_PATH = '/storage/emulated/0/RootBase/OutClaw/arena/data/arena_log.db' def get_connection(): # Enable WAL mode for concurrent multi-agent read/write safety conn = sqlite3.connect(DB_PATH, timeout=30.0) conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;") return conn def hash_file(file_path): sha256_hash = hashlib.sha256() if not os.path.exists(file_path): return "FILE_MISSING" with open(file_path, "rb") as f: for byte_block in iter(lambda: f.read(4096), b""): sha256_hash.update(byte_block) return sha256_hash.hexdigest() def register_run(round_num, device, agent, task_id, input_summary, output_path): out_hash = hash_file(output_path) conn = get_connection() cursor = conn.cursor() try: cursor.execute(''' INSERT INTO arena_runs (round, device, agent, task_id, input_summary, output_path, output_hash, status) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, 'completed') ''', (round_num, device, agent, task_id, input_summary, output_path, out_hash)) conn.commit() print(f"[Foresight Logger] Task {task_id} registered successfully. Hash: {out_hash[:8]}...") except Exception as e: conn.rollback() print(f"[Foresight Error] Failed to log task {task_id}: {e}") finally: conn.close() if __name__ == "__main__": print("Arena Runner Engine armed with WAL concurrency and SHA-256 verification.")
